## Summary: Lord Tickets Application

This is a **Hebrew travel/tourism business application** called "Lord Tickets" (מחולל הצעות מחיר - Quote Generator). It consists of three main components:

---

### 1. Main Web App (`index.html`)
A **price quote generator** for a travel agency or ticket business with:
- **Modern Hebrew RTL interface** with gold/navy branding
- Creates professional **travel proposals** including:
  - Flights
  - Hotels
  - Additional services
- **Automatic price calculations**
- Ability to save and manage multiple proposals
- Integration with Supabase for data storage
- Integration with Google Gemini API (AI features)

---

### 2. Gmail Image Extractor (`gmail-to-drive-script.js`)
A **Google Apps Script** that automates email processing:
- Monitors Gmail for emails tagged "LordTickets"
- Automatically extracts image attachments
- Saves them to Google Drive
- Creates a JSON manifest of all images
- Provides an API endpoint so the web app can import these images
- Runs automatically every 5 minutes

---

### 3. Flight Inventory Manager (`flight-inventory/app.py`)
A **Streamlit Python app** for managing flight inventory:
- Upload CSV/Excel inventory files
- Uses **Google Gemini AI** to extract flight info from text or screenshots
- Detects duplicate flights and updates existing entries
- Organizes flights by airline blocks
- Exports updated inventory as CSV with Hebrew support

---

This is a **travel agency toolkit** that helps create price quotes for customers, automatically collect images from emails, and manage flight inventory - all designed for Hebrew-speaking users (likely Israeli travel businesses).

Question: In the new web architecture, where should this heavy AI parsing logic live? Should I keep a Python microservice, or can we migrate this logic to a Supabase Edge Function (TypeScript) to keep the stack unified?

5. The Workflow Unification Challenge: I want to stop context-switching.

Question: How can we design the UI flow so that an agent can seamlessly toggle between "Building a Quote" and "Checking/Fixing Inventory" without losing unsaved data in the quote form?

## Current State Analysis

- [flight-inventory/app.py](flight-inventory/app.py) - Standalone Streamlit inventory manager

## Architecture Answers to Your 5 Challenges

### 1. Single Source of Truth - Preventing Double-Booking

**Recommended Pattern**: PostgreSQL atomic UPDATE with conditional check

```sql
-- Supabase stored procedure for atomic booking
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION book_seats(
  p_flight_id UUID,
  p_seats_requested INT,
  p_quote_id UUID
)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_affected INT;
BEGIN
  UPDATE flights
  SET 
    available_seats = available_seats - p_seats_requested,
    updated_at = NOW()
  WHERE id = p_flight_id
    AND available_seats >= p_seats_requested;
  
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_affected = ROW_COUNT;
  
  IF rows_affected = 0 THEN
    RETURN FALSE; -- Not enough seats
  END IF;
  
  -- Record the booking
  INSERT INTO bookings (quote_id, flight_id, seats_booked)
  VALUES (p_quote_id, p_flight_id, p_seats_requested);
  
  RETURN TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

**Why this works**: PostgreSQL executes the UPDATE atomically. The `WHERE available_seats >= p_seats_requested` clause ensures the decrement only happens if seats exist. No race condition possible.

**Schema Design**:

```mermaid
erDiagram
    FLIGHTS {
        uuid id PK
        date departure_date
        date return_date
        text outbound_details
        text inbound_details
        text airline
        text destination_code
        text destination_name
        int total_seats
        int available_seats
        decimal cost_price
        decimal selling_price
        text supplier_info
        timestamp created_at
        timestamp updated_at
    }
    
    QUOTES {
        uuid id PK
        text client_name
        text client_phone
        jsonb line_items
        decimal total_price
        text status
        timestamp expires_at
        timestamp created_at
    }
    
    QUOTE_FLIGHTS {
        uuid id PK
        uuid quote_id FK
        uuid flight_id FK
        int seats_requested
        decimal price_at_quote
    }
    
    BOOKINGS {
        uuid id PK
        uuid quote_id FK
        uuid flight_id FK
        int seats_booked
        timestamp booked_at
    }
    
    FLIGHTS ||--o{ QUOTE_FLIGHTS : "referenced in"
    QUOTES ||--o{ QUOTE_FLIGHTS : "contains"
    QUOTES ||--o{ BOOKINGS : "converts to"
    FLIGHTS ||--o{ BOOKINGS : "decrements"
```

### 2. Public Feed vs Business Secrets - Security Architecture

**Recommended Pattern**: Database VIEW + Edge Function API Gateway

Since you need **anonymous public access**, standard RLS is risky (anonymous role could potentially be misconfigured). The safest pattern:

**Layer 1 - PostgreSQL VIEW (defense in depth)**:
```sql
CREATE VIEW public_inventory AS
SELECT 
  id,
  departure_date,
  return_date,
  outbound_details,
  inbound_details,
  airline,
  destination_code,
  destination_name,
  available_seats,
  selling_price
  -- cost_price EXCLUDED
  -- supplier_info EXCLUDED
FROM flights
WHERE available_seats > 0;
```

**Layer 2 - Edge Function as API Gateway**:
```typescript
// supabase/functions/public-inventory/index.ts
Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const supabase = createClient(/* service role - internal only */);
  
  // Query ONLY the view, never the base table
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('public_inventory')
    .select('*');
  
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    headers: { 
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' 
    }
  });
});
```

**Why two layers**: Even if someone discovers your Supabase URL and anon key, they can only query the VIEW. The VIEW physically cannot return `cost_price` or `supplier_info` because those columns don't exist in it.

**RLS on base table** (for internal authenticated users):
```sql
-- Only authenticated users can see full flight data
ALTER TABLE flights ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Agents can view all flights"
ON flights FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (true);

CREATE POLICY "Agents can update flights"
ON flights FOR UPDATE
TO authenticated
USING (true);
```

### 3. Excel Replacement UI - Data Grid Recommendation

**Recommended: AG Grid Community Edition**

For 5,000+ rows with 2-5 concurrent users needing Excel-like editing:

| Library | Virtualization | Inline Edit | RTL Support | Learning Curve |
|---------|---------------|-------------|-------------|----------------|
| AG Grid Community | Excellent | Excellent | Built-in | Medium |
| TanStack Table | Manual setup | Manual | Manual | Higher |
| react-data-grid | Good | Good | Limited | Lower |

**AG Grid wins because**:
- Built-in virtualization handles 5,000+ rows smoothly
- Native inline cell editing with validation
- Hebrew RTL support built-in
- Clipboard paste from Excel
- Free for commercial use (Community Edition)

**Real-time sync pattern**:
```typescript
// Hook for real-time inventory updates
function useInventorySync() {
  const [flights, setFlights] = useState([]);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    // Initial load
    supabase.from('flights').select('*').then(({ data }) => setFlights(data));
    
    // Real-time subscription
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('flights-changes')
      .on('postgres_changes', 
        { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'flights' },
        (payload) => {
          // Merge changes into local state
          if (payload.eventType === 'UPDATE') {
            setFlights(prev => prev.map(f => 
              f.id === payload.new.id ? payload.new : f
            ));
          }
        }
      )
      .subscribe();
      
    return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel);
  }, []);
  
  return flights;
}
```

**Debounced cell edit handler**:
```typescript
const onCellValueChanged = useMemo(
  () => debounce(async (params) => {
    const { data, colDef } = params;
    await supabase
      .from('flights')
      .update({ [colDef.field]: data[colDef.field] })
      .eq('id', data.id);
  }, 300),
  []
);
```

---

### 4. AI Parsing Location - Recommendation

**Recommended: Supabase Edge Function (TypeScript)**

Since AI parsing is rare (few times per day), a unified TypeScript stack is simpler:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/parse-flight/index.ts
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from '@google/generative-ai';

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const { text, imageBase64 } = await req.json();
  
  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(Deno.env.get('GEMINI_API_KEY'));
  const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: 'gemini-1.5-flash' });
  
  const prompt = `Extract flight information... (same prompt from app.py)`;
  
  const result = imageBase64
    ? await model.generateContent([prompt, { inlineData: { data: imageBase64, mimeType: 'image/jpeg' }}])
    : await model.generateContent(`${prompt}\n\nInput:\n${text}`);
  
  const parsed = JSON.parse(result.response.text());
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(parsed));
});
```

**Why Edge Function over Python microservice**:
- No separate infrastructure to deploy/maintain
- Same Supabase project, unified billing
- TypeScript shares types with frontend
- For rare operations, cold start latency (~200ms) is acceptable

**Keep Python option open**: If you later need bulk parsing (hundreds of flights), you can add a Python Cloud Run service without changing the frontend - just point the Edge Function to the Python service as a proxy.

---

### 5. Workflow Unification - UI Design

**Recommended Pattern: Persistent State + Slide-out Panel**

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    subgraph MainView [Main View]
        QuoteBuilder[Quote Builder Form]
    end
    
    subgraph SlidePanel [Slide-out Panel]
        InventoryGrid[Inventory Grid]
        QuickEdit[Quick Edit Mode]
    end
    
    QuoteBuilder -->|"Click 'Check Inventory'"| SlidePanel
    SlidePanel -->|"Select Flight"| QuoteBuilder
    
    subgraph StateManager [Zustand Store]
        DraftQuote[Draft Quote State]
        InventoryCache[Inventory Cache]
    end
    
    QuoteBuilder <--> DraftQuote
    InventoryGrid <--> InventoryCache
```

**Implementation approach**:

1. **Zustand store** for quote draft persistence:
```typescript
const useQuoteStore = create((set) => ({
  draft: null,
  setDraft: (draft) => set({ draft }),
  // Auto-persist to localStorage
  ...persist({ name: 'quote-draft' })
}));
```

2. **Slide-out inventory panel** (doesn't unmount quote form):
```tsx
function App() {
  const [inventoryOpen, setInventoryOpen] = useState(false);
  
  return (
    <>
      <QuoteBuilder /> {/* Always mounted, state preserved */}
      
      <SlideOutPanel open={inventoryOpen} onClose={() => setInventoryOpen(false)}>
        <InventoryGrid 
          onSelectFlight={(flight) => {
            useQuoteStore.getState().addFlightToQuote(flight);
            setInventoryOpen(false);
          }}
        />
      </SlideOutPanel>
    </>
  );
}
```

3. **Quick actions from inventory**: Add "Add to Quote" button in each grid row that pushes the flight to the current draft without navigation.

## Current State Analysis

Your system has three disconnected parts:
- [index.html](index.html) - Monolithic HTML/JS quote generator (~9000 lines)
- [flight-inventory/app.py](flight-inventory/app.py) - Standalone Streamlit inventory manager
- [gmail-to-drive-script.js](gmail-to-drive-script.js) - Google Apps Script for image extraction

**Key Gap**: Quotes stored as HTML blobs with no reference to inventory records. No transactional integrity between sales and inventory.

---

## Architecture Answers to Your 5 Challenges

### 1. Single Source of Truth - Preventing Double-Booking

**Recommended Pattern**: PostgreSQL atomic UPDATE with conditional check

```sql
-- Supabase stored procedure for atomic booking
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION book_seats(
  p_flight_id UUID,
  p_seats_requested INT,
  p_quote_id UUID
)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_affected INT;
BEGIN
  UPDATE flights
  SET 
    available_seats = available_seats - p_seats_requested,
    updated_at = NOW()
  WHERE id = p_flight_id
    AND available_seats >= p_seats_requested;
  
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_affected = ROW_COUNT;
  
  IF rows_affected = 0 THEN
    RETURN FALSE; -- Not enough seats
  END IF;
  
  -- Record the booking
  INSERT INTO bookings (quote_id, flight_id, seats_booked)
  VALUES (p_quote_id, p_flight_id, p_seats_requested);
  
  RETURN TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

**Why this works**: PostgreSQL executes the UPDATE atomically. The `WHERE available_seats >= p_seats_requested` clause ensures the decrement only happens if seats exist. No race condition possible.

**Schema Design**:

```mermaid
erDiagram
    FLIGHTS {
        uuid id PK
        date departure_date
        date return_date
        text outbound_details
        text inbound_details
        text airline
        text destination_code
        text destination_name
        int total_seats
        int available_seats
        decimal cost_price
        decimal selling_price
        text supplier_info
        timestamp created_at
        timestamp updated_at
    }
    
    QUOTES {
        uuid id PK
        text client_name
        text client_phone
        jsonb line_items
        decimal total_price
        text status
        timestamp expires_at
        timestamp created_at
    }
    
    QUOTE_FLIGHTS {
        uuid id PK
        uuid quote_id FK
        uuid flight_id FK
        int seats_requested
        decimal price_at_quote
    }
    
    BOOKINGS {
        uuid id PK
        uuid quote_id FK
        uuid flight_id FK
        int seats_booked
        timestamp booked_at
    }
    
    FLIGHTS ||--o{ QUOTE_FLIGHTS : "referenced in"
    QUOTES ||--o{ QUOTE_FLIGHTS : "contains"
    QUOTES ||--o{ BOOKINGS : "converts to"
    FLIGHTS ||--o{ BOOKINGS : "decrements"
```

### 2. Public Feed vs Business Secrets - Security Architecture

**Recommended Pattern**: Database VIEW + Edge Function API Gateway

Since you need **anonymous public access**, standard RLS is risky (anonymous role could potentially be misconfigured). The safest pattern:

**Layer 1 - PostgreSQL VIEW (defense in depth)**:
```sql
CREATE VIEW public_inventory AS
SELECT 
  id,
  departure_date,
  return_date,
  outbound_details,
  inbound_details,
  airline,
  destination_code,
  destination_name,
  available_seats,
  selling_price
  -- cost_price EXCLUDED
  -- supplier_info EXCLUDED
FROM flights
WHERE available_seats > 0;
```

**Layer 2 - Edge Function as API Gateway**:
```typescript
// supabase/functions/public-inventory/index.ts
Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const supabase = createClient(/* service role - internal only */);
  
  // Query ONLY the view, never the base table
  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('public_inventory')
    .select('*');
  
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    headers: { 
      'Content-Type': 'application/json',
      'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' 
    }
  });
});
```

**Why two layers**: Even if someone discovers your Supabase URL and anon key, they can only query the VIEW. The VIEW physically cannot return `cost_price` or `supplier_info` because those columns don't exist in it.

**RLS on base table** (for internal authenticated users):
```sql
-- Only authenticated users can see full flight data
ALTER TABLE flights ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Agents can view all flights"
ON flights FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (true);

CREATE POLICY "Agents can update flights"
ON flights FOR UPDATE
TO authenticated
USING (true);
```

---

### 3. Excel Replacement UI - Data Grid Recommendation

**Recommended: AG Grid Community Edition**

For 5,000+ rows with 2-5 concurrent users needing Excel-like editing:

| Library | Virtualization | Inline Edit | RTL Support | Learning Curve |
|---------|---------------|-------------|-------------|----------------|
| AG Grid Community | Excellent | Excellent | Built-in | Medium |
| TanStack Table | Manual setup | Manual | Manual | Higher |
| react-data-grid | Good | Good | Limited | Lower |

**AG Grid wins because**:
- Built-in virtualization handles 5,000+ rows smoothly
- Native inline cell editing with validation
- Hebrew RTL support built-in
- Clipboard paste from Excel
- Free for commercial use (Community Edition)

**Real-time sync pattern**:
```typescript
// Hook for real-time inventory updates
function useInventorySync() {
  const [flights, setFlights] = useState([]);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    // Initial load
    supabase.from('flights').select('*').then(({ data }) => setFlights(data));
    
    // Real-time subscription
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('flights-changes')
      .on('postgres_changes', 
        { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'flights' },
        (payload) => {
          // Merge changes into local state
          if (payload.eventType === 'UPDATE') {
            setFlights(prev => prev.map(f => 
              f.id === payload.new.id ? payload.new : f
            ));
          }
        }
      )
      .subscribe();
      
    return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel);
  }, []);
  
  return flights;
}
```

**Debounced cell edit handler**:
```typescript
const onCellValueChanged = useMemo(
  () => debounce(async (params) => {
    const { data, colDef } = params;
    await supabase
      .from('flights')
      .update({ [colDef.field]: data[colDef.field] })
      .eq('id', data.id);
  }, 300),
  []
);
```

---

### 4. AI Parsing Location - Recommendation

**Recommended: Supabase Edge Function (TypeScript)**

Since AI parsing is rare (few times per day), a unified TypeScript stack is simpler:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/parse-flight/index.ts
import { GoogleGenerativeAI } from '@google/generative-ai';

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const { text, imageBase64 } = await req.json();
  
  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(Deno.env.get('GEMINI_API_KEY'));
  const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: 'gemini-1.5-flash' });
  
  const prompt = `Extract flight information... (same prompt from app.py)`;
  
  const result = imageBase64
    ? await model.generateContent([prompt, { inlineData: { data: imageBase64, mimeType: 'image/jpeg' }}])
    : await model.generateContent(`${prompt}\n\nInput:\n${text}`);
  
  const parsed = JSON.parse(result.response.text());
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(parsed));
});
```

**Why Edge Function over Python microservice**:
- No separate infrastructure to deploy/maintain
- Same Supabase project, unified billing
- TypeScript shares types with frontend
- For rare operations, cold start latency (~200ms) is acceptable

**Keep Python option open**: If you later need bulk parsing (hundreds of flights), you can add a Python Cloud Run service without changing the frontend - just point the Edge Function to the Python service as a proxy.

### 5. Workflow Unification - UI Design

**Recommended Pattern: Persistent State + Slide-out Panel**

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    subgraph MainView [Main View]
        QuoteBuilder[Quote Builder Form]
    end
    
    subgraph SlidePanel [Slide-out Panel]
        InventoryGrid[Inventory Grid]
        QuickEdit[Quick Edit Mode]
    end
    
    QuoteBuilder -->|"Click 'Check Inventory'"| SlidePanel
    SlidePanel -->|"Select Flight"| QuoteBuilder
    
    subgraph StateManager [Zustand Store]
        DraftQuote[Draft Quote State]
        InventoryCache[Inventory Cache]
    end
    
    QuoteBuilder <--> DraftQuote
    InventoryGrid <--> InventoryCache
```

**Implementation approach**:

1. **Zustand store** for quote draft persistence:
```typescript
const useQuoteStore = create((set) => ({
  draft: null,
  setDraft: (draft) => set({ draft }),
  // Auto-persist to localStorage
  ...persist({ name: 'quote-draft' })
}));
```

2. **Slide-out inventory panel** (doesn't unmount quote form):
```tsx
function App() {
  const [inventoryOpen, setInventoryOpen] = useState(false);
  
  return (
    <>
      <QuoteBuilder /> {/* Always mounted, state preserved */}
      
      <SlideOutPanel open={inventoryOpen} onClose={() => setInventoryOpen(false)}>
        <InventoryGrid 
          onSelectFlight={(flight) => {
            useQuoteStore.getState().addFlightToQuote(flight);
            setInventoryOpen(false);
          }}
        />
      </SlideOutPanel>
    </>
  );
}
```

3. **Quick actions from inventory**: Add "Add to Quote" button in each grid row that pushes the flight to the current draft without navigation.

## Core Implementation

### 1. Atomic Booking (Prevents Double-Booking)

PostgreSQL stored procedure ensures race-condition-free seat decrement:

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION book_seats(
  p_flight_id UUID,
  p_seats_requested INT,
  p_quote_id UUID
) RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_affected INT;
BEGIN
  -- Atomic: only decrements if seats available
  UPDATE flights
  SET available_seats = available_seats - p_seats_requested,
      updated_at = NOW()
  WHERE id = p_flight_id
    AND available_seats >= p_seats_requested;
  
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_affected = ROW_COUNT;
  
  IF rows_affected = 0 THEN
    RETURN FALSE;
  END IF;
  
  INSERT INTO bookings (quote_id, flight_id, seats_booked)
  VALUES (p_quote_id, p_flight_id, p_seats_requested);
  
  RETURN TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

### 2. Security Architecture (Public API with Hidden Costs)

Two-layer protection ensures `cost_price` and `supplier_info` are physically impossible to query externally:

**Layer 1 - Database VIEW** (columns don't exist):
```sql
CREATE VIEW public_inventory AS
SELECT id, departure_date, return_date, outbound_details, inbound_details,
       airline, destination_code, destination_name, available_seats, selling_price
FROM flights WHERE available_seats > 0;
-- cost_price and supplier_info EXCLUDED from view
```

**Layer 2 - Edge Function Gateway**:
```typescript
// supabase/functions/public-inventory/index.ts
Deno.serve(async () => {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('public_inventory').select('*');
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' }
  });
});
```

**Layer 3 - RLS for Authenticated Users**:
```sql
ALTER TABLE flights ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Agents full access" ON flights FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (true);
```

### 3. Excel-Like Inventory Grid

AG Grid Community with real-time Supabase sync:

```typescript
// useInventorySync.ts
function useInventorySync() {
  const [flights, setFlights] = useState([]);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.from('flights').select('*').then(({ data }) => setFlights(data));
    
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('flights-changes')
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'flights' },
        (payload) => {
          if (payload.eventType === 'UPDATE') {
            setFlights(prev => prev.map(f => f.id === payload.new.id ? payload.new : f));
          }
        })
      .subscribe();
      
    return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel);
  }, []);
  
  return flights;
}

// Debounced save on cell edit
const onCellValueChanged = debounce(async (params) => {
  await supabase.from('flights')
    .update({ [params.colDef.field]: params.data[params.colDef.field] })
    .eq('id', params.data.id);
}, 300);
```

### 4. AI Flight Parser (Edge Function)

Gemini-powered text/image parsing in TypeScript:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/parse-flight/index.ts
Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const { text, imageBase64 } = await req.json();
  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(Deno.env.get('GEMINI_API_KEY'));
  const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: 'gemini-1.5-flash' });
  
  const prompt = `Extract: dates, outbound, inbound, airline, destination, seats, price. Return JSON only.`;
  
  const result = imageBase64
    ? await model.generateContent([prompt, { inlineData: { data: imageBase64, mimeType: 'image/jpeg' }}])
    : await model.generateContent(`${prompt}\n\n${text}`);
  
  return new Response(result.response.text());
});
```

### 5. Unified Workflow UI

Slide-out panel preserves quote state while checking inventory:

```tsx
function App() {
  const [inventoryOpen, setInventoryOpen] = useState(false);
  
  return (
    <>
      <QuoteBuilder /> {/* Always mounted - state preserved */}
      
      <SlideOutPanel open={inventoryOpen} onClose={() => setInventoryOpen(false)}>
        <InventoryGrid onSelectFlight={(flight) => {
          useQuoteStore.getState().addFlightToQuote(flight);
          setInventoryOpen(false);
        }} />
      </SlideOutPanel>
    </>
  );
}

// Zustand store with localStorage persistence
const useQuoteStore = create(persist((set) => ({
  draft: null,
  setDraft: (draft) => set({ draft }),
  addFlightToQuote: (flight) => set(state => ({
    draft: { ...state.draft, flights: [...(state.draft?.flights || []), flight] }
  }))
}), { name: 'quote-draft' }));
```

## Tech Stack Summary

| Layer | Technology | Why |
|-------|------------|-----|
| Frontend | React + Vite + TypeScript | Modern, fast DX |
| UI Components | AG Grid Community | Excel-like editing, 5000+ row virtualization, RTL support |
| State | Zustand + persist | Simple, localStorage backup for drafts |
| Backend | Supabase | Auth, DB, Realtime, Edge Functions in one |
| Database | PostgreSQL | Atomic transactions, stored procedures |
| Security | Views + RLS + Edge Functions | Three-layer protection for sensitive data |
| AI | Gemini via Edge Function | Unified TypeScript stack, no Python needed |

# Lord Tickets - Unified Architecture

## System Overview

A single React + Supabase application replacing three disconnected systems:

- [index.html](index.html) - Quote generator (9000+ lines)
- [flight-inventory/app.py](flight-inventory/app.py) - Streamlit inventory manager  
- [gmail-to-drive-script.js](gmail-to-drive-script.js) - Image automation

```mermaid
        DB[(PostgreSQL)]

    InventoryGrid --> DB
```

## Core Implementation

### 1. Atomic Booking (Prevents Double-Booking)

PostgreSQL stored procedure ensures race-condition-free seat decrement:

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION book_seats(
  p_flight_id UUID,
  p_seats_requested INT,
  p_quote_id UUID
) RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_affected INT;
BEGIN
  -- Atomic: only decrements if seats available
  UPDATE flights
  SET available_seats = available_seats - p_seats_requested,
      updated_at = NOW()
  WHERE id = p_flight_id
    AND available_seats >= p_seats_requested;
  
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_affected = ROW_COUNT;
  
  IF rows_affected = 0 THEN
    RETURN FALSE;
  END IF;
  
  INSERT INTO bookings (quote_id, flight_id, seats_booked)
  VALUES (p_quote_id, p_flight_id, p_seats_requested);
  
  RETURN TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

### 2. Security Architecture (Public API with Hidden Costs)

Two-layer protection ensures `cost_price` and `supplier_info` are physically impossible to query externally:

**Layer 1 - Database VIEW** (columns don't exist):

```sql
CREATE VIEW public_inventory AS
SELECT id, departure_date, return_date, outbound_details, inbound_details,
       airline, destination_code, destination_name, available_seats, selling_price
FROM flights WHERE available_seats > 0;
-- cost_price and supplier_info EXCLUDED from view
```

**Layer 2 - Edge Function Gateway**:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/public-inventory/index.ts
Deno.serve(async () => {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('public_inventory').select('*');
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' }
  });
});
```

**Layer 3 - RLS for Authenticated Users**:

```sql
ALTER TABLE flights ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "Agents full access" ON flights FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (true);
```

### 3. Excel-Like Inventory Grid

AG Grid Community with real-time Supabase sync:

```typescript
// useInventorySync.ts
function useInventorySync() {
  const [flights, setFlights] = useState([]);
  
  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.from('flights').select('*').then(({ data }) => setFlights(data));
    
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('flights-changes')
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'flights' },
        (payload) => {
          if (payload.eventType === 'UPDATE') {
            setFlights(prev => prev.map(f => f.id === payload.new.id ? payload.new : f));
          }
        })
      .subscribe();
      
    return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel);
  }, []);
  
  return flights;
}

// Debounced save on cell edit
const onCellValueChanged = debounce(async (params) => {
  await supabase.from('flights')
    .update({ [params.colDef.field]: params.data[params.colDef.field] })
    .eq('id', params.data.id);
}, 300);
```

### 4. AI Flight Parser (Edge Function)

Gemini-powered text/image parsing in TypeScript:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/parse-flight/index.ts
Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const { text, imageBase64 } = await req.json();
  const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(Deno.env.get('GEMINI_API_KEY'));
  const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: 'gemini-1.5-flash' });
  
  const prompt = `Extract: dates, outbound, inbound, airline, destination, seats, price. Return JSON only.`;
  
  const result = imageBase64
    ? await model.generateContent([prompt, { inlineData: { data: imageBase64, mimeType: 'image/jpeg' }}])
    : await model.generateContent(`${prompt}\n\n${text}`);
  
  return new Response(result.response.text());
});
```

### 5. Unified Workflow UI

Slide-out panel preserves quote state while checking inventory:

```tsx
function App() {
  const [inventoryOpen, setInventoryOpen] = useState(false);
  
  return (
    <>
      <QuoteBuilder /> {/* Always mounted - state preserved */}
      
      <SlideOutPanel open={inventoryOpen} onClose={() => setInventoryOpen(false)}>
        <InventoryGrid onSelectFlight={(flight) => {
          useQuoteStore.getState().addFlightToQuote(flight);
          setInventoryOpen(false);
        }} />
      </SlideOutPanel>
    </>
  );
}

// Zustand store with localStorage persistence
const useQuoteStore = create(persist((set) => ({
  draft: null,
  setDraft: (draft) => set({ draft }),
  addFlightToQuote: (flight) => set(state => ({
    draft: { ...state.draft, flights: [...(state.draft?.flights || []), flight] }
  }))
}), { name: 'quote-draft' }));
```

| Layer | Technology | Why |

|-------|------------|-----|

| Frontend | React + Vite + TypeScript | Modern, fast DX |

| UI Components | AG Grid Community | Excel-like editing, 5000+ row virtualization, RTL support |

| State | Zustand + persist | Simple, localStorage backup for drafts |

| Backend | Supabase | Auth, DB, Realtime, Edge Functions in one |

| Database | PostgreSQL | Atomic transactions, stored procedures |

| Security | Views + RLS + Edge Functions | Three-layer protection for sensitive data |

| AI | Gemini via Edge Function | Unified TypeScript stack, no Python needed |

## Core Implementation

### 1. Atomic Booking (Prevents Double-Booking)

```sql
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION book_seats(
  p_flight_id UUID,
  p_seats_requested INT,
  p_quote_id UUID
) RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
DECLARE
  rows_affected INT;
BEGIN
  UPDATE flights
  SET available_seats = available_seats - p_seats_requested,
      updated_at = NOW()
  WHERE id = p_flight_id
    AND available_seats >= p_seats_requested;
  
  GET DIAGNOSTICS rows_affected = ROW_COUNT;
  
  IF rows_affected = 0 THEN
    RETURN FALSE;
  END IF;
  
  INSERT INTO bookings (quote_id, flight_id, seats_booked)
  VALUES (p_quote_id, p_flight_id, p_seats_requested);
  
  RETURN TRUE;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;
```

### 2. Security Architecture (Three Layers)

**Layer 1 - Database VIEW** (private columns physically excluded):
```sql
CREATE VIEW public_inventory AS
SELECT id, departure_date, return_date, outbound_details, inbound_details,
       airline, destination_code, destination_name, available_seats, selling_price
FROM flights WHERE available_seats > 0;
```

**Layer 2 - Edge Function Gateway**:

```typescript
// supabase/functions/public-inventory/index.ts
Deno.serve(async () => {
  const { data } = await supabase.from('public_inventory').select('*');
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {
```

### 3. AI Parser with Confidence Scores

```typescript
// supabase/functions/parse-flight/index.ts
interface ParsedField {
  value: string | number;
  confidence: number; // 0.0 - 1.0
}

interface ParsedFlight {
  dates: ParsedField;
  outbound: ParsedField;
  inbound: ParsedField;
  airline: ParsedField;
  destination: ParsedField;
  seats: ParsedField;
  price: ParsedField;
}

const PROMPT = `Extract flight information. Return JSON:
{
  "dates": {"value": "DD/MM/YY - DD/MM/YY", "confidence": 0.0-1.0},
  "outbound": {"value": "HH:MM CITY (CODE) -> HH:MM CITY (CODE) FLIGHT#", "confidence": 0.0-1.0},
  "inbound": {"value": "same format", "confidence": 0.0-1.0},
  "airline": {"value": "Name", "confidence": 0.0-1.0},
  "destination": {"value": "City (CODE)", "confidence": 0.0-1.0},
  "seats": {"value": number, "confidence": 0.0-1.0},
  "price": {"value": "123$", "confidence": 0.0-1.0}
}
Rules:
- If arrival time < departure time, add (+1) to arrival
- Always use DD/MM/YY format
- If multiple flights found, return array`;

Deno.serve(async (req) => {
  const { text, imageBase64 } = await req.json();
  
  // Retry logic with exponential backoff
  const result = await withRetry(async () => {
    const genAI = new GoogleGenerativeAI(Deno.env.get('GEMINI_API_KEY'));
    const model = genAI.getGenerativeModel({ model: 'gemini-1.5-flash' });
    
    const content = imageBase64
      ? [PROMPT, { inlineData: { data: imageBase64, mimeType: 'image/jpeg' }}]
      : `${PROMPT}\n\nInput:\n${text}`;
    
    return await model.generateContent(content);
  }, { maxAttempts: 3, delayMs: 1000 });
  
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(parseResponse(result)));
});
```

### AI Parser Modal (with Confidence Indicators)

```
+------------------------------------------------------------------+
| PARSE FLIGHT DATA                                       [X Close] |
+------------------------------------------------------------------+
| INPUT                                                             |
| +--------------------------------------------------------------+ |
```

## Error Handling System

### Error Codes and Recovery

```typescript
// src/lib/errors.ts
enum ErrorCode {
  // API Errors (E0xx)
  API_BAD_JSON = 'E001',
  API_RATE_LIMIT = 'E002',
  API_TOKEN_LIMIT = 'E003',
  API_TIMEOUT = 'E004',
  
  // Input Errors (E1xx)
  INPUT_BLURRY = 'E101',
  INPUT_MULTI_FLIGHT = 'E103',
  INPUT_MISSING_FIELDS = 'E104',
  
  // File Errors (E2xx)
  FILE_CORRUPTED = 'E201',
  FILE_WRONG_COLUMNS = 'E202',
  
  // Booking Errors (E3xx)
  BOOKING_NO_SEATS = 'E301',
  BOOKING_CONFLICT = 'E302',
}

const ERROR_HANDLERS: Record<ErrorCode, ErrorHandler> = {
  [ErrorCode.API_BAD_JSON]: {
    message: 'AI response was unclear',
    action: 'Retrying with stricter prompt...',
    recovery: async () => retryWithStrictPrompt(),
    retryable: true,
  },
  [ErrorCode.API_RATE_LIMIT]: {
    message: 'Too many requests',
    action: 'Please wait {seconds} seconds',
    recovery: async (ctx) => waitAndRetry(ctx.retryAfter),
    retryable: true,
  },
  [ErrorCode.BOOKING_NO_SEATS]: {
    message: 'Not enough seats available',
    action: 'Another agent may have booked these seats',
    recovery: async () => refreshInventory(),
    retryable: false,
  },
  // ... etc
};
```

### Error Flow Diagram

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    subgraph api [API Errors]
        A1[Bad JSON] -->|Auto retry 3x| A2{Success?}
        A2 -->|No| A3[Show manual form]
        A2 -->|Yes| A4[Continue]
        
        B1[Rate Limit] --> B2[Show countdown]
        B2 --> B3[Auto-retry]
        
        C1[Timeout] --> C2[Retry button]
    end
    
    subgraph input [Input Errors]
        D1[Low confidence] --> D2[Highlight fields]
        D2 --> D3[Require user edit]
        
        E1[Duplicate found] --> E2[Show options]
        E2 --> E3{User choice}
        E3 -->|Update| E4[Merge data]
        E3 -->|New| E5[Add row]
        E3 -->|Skip| E6[Cancel]
    end
    
    subgraph booking [Booking Errors]
        F1[No seats] --> F2[Refresh inventory]
        F2 --> F3[Show updated availability]
    end
```

## Offline Support

```typescript
// src/lib/offline.ts
import { openDB } from 'idb';

const db = await openDB('lord-tickets', 1, {
  upgrade(db) {
    db.createObjectStore('flights', { keyPath: 'id' });
    db.createObjectStore('pendingChanges', { keyPath: 'id', autoIncrement: true });
    db.createObjectStore('quotes', { keyPath: 'id' });
  },
});

// Cache flights for offline access
async function cacheFlights(flights: Flight[]) {
  const tx = db.transaction('flights', 'readwrite');
  await Promise.all(flights.map(f => tx.store.put(f)));
}

// Queue changes when offline
async function queueChange(change: PendingChange) {
  await db.put('pendingChanges', {
    ...change,
    timestamp: Date.now(),
  });
}

// Sync when back online
window.addEventListener('online', async () => {
  const pending = await db.getAll('pendingChanges');
  for (const change of pending) {
    await applyChange(change);
    await db.delete('pendingChanges', change.id);
  }
});
```

---

## Undo/Redo System

```typescript
// src/stores/historyStore.ts
interface HistoryState {
  past: FlightState[];
  present: FlightState;
  future: FlightState[];
}

const useHistoryStore = create<HistoryState>((set, get) => ({
  past: [],
  present: initialState,
  future: [],
  
  push: (newState: FlightState) => set(state => ({
    past: [...state.past.slice(-20), state.present], // Keep last 20
    present: newState,
    future: [],
  })),
  
  undo: () => set(state => {
    if (state.past.length === 0) return state;
    const previous = state.past[state.past.length - 1];
    return {
      past: state.past.slice(0, -1),
      present: previous,
      future: [state.present, ...state.future],
    };
  }),
  
  redo: () => set(state => {
    if (state.future.length === 0) return state;
    const next = state.future[0];
    return {
      past: [...state.past, state.present],
      present: next,
      future: state.future.slice(1),
    };
  }),
  
  canUndo: () => get().past.length > 0,
  canRedo: () => get().future.length > 0,
}));
```

## Airline Normalization

```typescript
// src/lib/airlines.ts
const AIRLINE_ALIASES: Record<string, string> = {
  'wizz': 'Wizz Air',
  'wizz air': 'Wizz Air',
  'wizzair': 'Wizz Air',
  'w6': 'Wizz Air',
  'ryanair': 'Ryanair',
  'ryan air': 'Ryanair',
  'fr': 'Ryanair',
  'easyjet': 'easyJet',
  'easy jet': 'easyJet',
  'u2': 'easyJet',
  'elal': 'El Al',
  'el al': 'El Al',
  'ly': 'El Al',
};

export function normalizeAirline(name: string): string {
  const lower = name.toLowerCase().trim();
  return AIRLINE_ALIASES[lower] || name.trim();
}
```

---

## Tech Stack

| Layer | Technology | Purpose |
|-------|------------|---------|
| Frontend | React 18 + Vite + TypeScript | Modern SPA framework |
| UI Grid | AG Grid Community | Excel-like editing, 5000+ row virtualization |
| State | Zustand + persist | Simple state with localStorage backup |
| Offline | Service Worker + IndexedDB (idb) | Offline-first capability |
| Backend | Supabase | Auth, DB, Realtime, Edge Functions |
| Database | PostgreSQL | Atomic transactions, stored procedures |
| Security | Views + RLS + Edge Functions | Three-layer data protection |
| AI | Gemini 1.5 Flash | Flight text/image parsing |
| Styling | CSS Variables + RTL | Gold/Navy theme, Hebrew support |

---

## Implementation Phases

1. **Phase 1 - Foundation**
   - Create Supabase project with schema
   - Initialize React + Vite + TypeScript
   - Set up AG Grid with basic CRUD

2. **Phase 2 - Core Features**
   - Real-time sync with Supabase subscriptions
   - AI parser Edge Function with confidence scores
   - Undo/redo system

3. **Phase 3 - Quote Builder**
   - Port quote builder from index.html
   - Slide-out inventory panel
   - PDF/WhatsApp export

4. **Phase 4 - Booking & Security**
   - Atomic booking stored procedure
   - Public API with VIEW protection
   - RLS policies

5. **Phase 5 - Polish**
   - Offline support (Service Worker + IndexedDB)
   - Error handling with recovery
   - CSV import with column mapping

6. **Phase 6 - Migration**
   - Import existing CSV data
   - User testing
   - Retire legacy Python app and HTML files

---

## Testing Checklist

Before deployment, verify these scenarios:

**API/AI Errors**
- [ ] Malformed JSON response (auto-retry)
- [ ] Rate limit handling (countdown + retry)
- [ ] Image too large (compression)
- [ ] Network timeout (retry button)

**Input Quality**
- [ ] Blurry image (warning + proceed)
- [ ] Multiple flights in one image (selection UI)
- [ ] Missing required fields (highlight + block)
- [ ] Low confidence fields (require edit)

**Data Integrity**
- [ ] Duplicate flight detection
- [ ] Concurrent booking (race condition)
- [ ] Offline changes sync correctly

**CSV Import**
- [ ] Wrong column names (mapping UI)
- [ ] Mixed encodings (auto-detect)
- [ ] 5000+ row import (chunked)

[Tool call] Write
  path: c:\Users\shake\lord-tickets-app\supabase\migrations\001_create_tables.sql
  contents: -- Lord Tickets - Database Schema
-- Migration 001: Create core tables

-- Enable UUID extension
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- ============================================
-- FLIGHTS TABLE (Master Inventory)
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE flights (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    departure_date DATE NOT NULL,
    return_date DATE,
    outbound_details TEXT NOT NULL,
    inbound_details TEXT,
    airline TEXT NOT NULL,
    destination_code TEXT NOT NULL,
    destination_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    total_seats INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    available_seats INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    cost_price DECIMAL(10, 2),          -- PRIVATE: Hidden from public API
    selling_price DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    supplier_info TEXT,                  -- PRIVATE: Hidden from public API
    notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Index for common queries
CREATE INDEX idx_flights_departure ON flights(departure_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_flights_airline ON flights(airline);
CREATE INDEX idx_flights_destination ON flights(destination_code);
CREATE INDEX idx_flights_available ON flights(available_seats) WHERE available_seats > 0;

-- ============================================
-- QUOTES TABLE
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE quotes (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    client_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    client_phone TEXT,
    client_email TEXT,
    total_cost DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    total_price DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT 0,
    markup_percent DECIMAL(5, 2) DEFAULT 15,
    status TEXT DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft', 'sent', 'accepted', 'rejected', 'expired')),
    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_quotes_status ON quotes(status);
CREATE INDEX idx_quotes_client ON quotes(client_name);

-- ============================================
-- QUOTE_FLIGHTS TABLE (Junction table)
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE quote_flights (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    quote_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES quotes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    flight_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES flights(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    seats_requested INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    price_at_quote DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_quote_flights_quote ON quote_flights(quote_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_quote_flights_flight ON quote_flights(flight_id);

-- ============================================
-- BOOKINGS TABLE (Confirmed sales)
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE bookings (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    quote_id UUID REFERENCES quotes(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    flight_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES flights(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    seats_booked INTEGER NOT NULL,
    client_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    client_phone TEXT,
    total_paid DECIMAL(10, 2),
    payment_status TEXT DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (payment_status IN ('pending', 'partial', 'paid', 'refunded')),
    booked_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_bookings_flight ON bookings(flight_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_bookings_quote ON bookings(quote_id);

-- ============================================
-- AUDIT_LOG TABLE (Track changes)
-- ============================================
CREATE TABLE audit_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    user_id UUID,
    user_email TEXT,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    table_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    record_id UUID,
    old_values JSONB,
    new_values JSONB,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

CREATE INDEX idx_audit_table ON audit_log(table_name);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_record ON audit_log(record_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_created ON audit_log(created_at);

-- ============================================
-- UPDATE TIMESTAMP TRIGGER
-- ============================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at = NOW();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER flights_updated_at
    BEFORE UPDATE ON flights
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at();

CREATE TRIGGER quotes_updated_at
    BEFORE UPDATE ON quotes
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at();

  path: c:\Users\shake\lord-tickets-app\supabase\migrations\002_public_view.sql
  contents: -- Lord Tickets - Public Inventory View
-- Migration 002: Create VIEW that excludes sensitive columns

-- ============================================
-- PUBLIC INVENTORY VIEW
-- Exposes only public data, excludes:
--   - cost_price
--   - supplier_info
-- ============================================
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public_inventory AS
SELECT 
    id,
    departure_date,
    return_date,
    outbound_details,
    inbound_details,
    airline,
    destination_code,
    destination_name,
    available_seats,
    selling_price,
    created_at,
    updated_at
FROM flights
WHERE available_seats > 0
ORDER BY departure_date ASC;

-- Comment for documentation
COMMENT ON VIEW public_inventory IS 'Public-facing inventory view. Excludes cost_price and supplier_info for security.';

[Tool call] Write
  path: c:\Users\shake\lord-tickets-app\supabase\migrations\003_booking_function.sql
  contents: -- Lord Tickets - Booking Functions
-- Migration 003: Atomic booking stored procedure

-- ============================================
-- BOOK_SEATS FUNCTION
-- Atomically decrements seats and creates booking
-- Returns: success boolean, message text
-- ============================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION book_seats(
    p_flight_id UUID,
    p_seats_requested INTEGER,
    p_quote_id UUID DEFAULT NULL,
    p_client_name TEXT DEFAULT 'Guest',
    p_client_phone TEXT DEFAULT NULL
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    success BOOLEAN,
    message TEXT,
    booking_id UUID
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_rows_affected INTEGER;
    v_booking_id UUID;
    v_available INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Check current availability first (for better error message)
    SELECT available_seats INTO v_available
    FROM flights
    WHERE id = p_flight_id;
    
    IF v_available IS NULL THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT FALSE, 'Flight not found'::TEXT, NULL::UUID;
        RETURN;
    END IF;
    
    IF v_available < p_seats_requested THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT FALSE, 
            format('Only %s seats available (requested %s)', v_available, p_seats_requested)::TEXT, 
            NULL::UUID;
        RETURN;
    END IF;
    
    -- Atomic update with conditional check
    UPDATE flights
    SET 
        available_seats = available_seats - p_seats_requested,
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = p_flight_id
      AND available_seats >= p_seats_requested;
    
    GET DIAGNOSTICS v_rows_affected = ROW_COUNT;
    
    IF v_rows_affected = 0 THEN
        -- Race condition: seats were taken between check and update
        RETURN QUERY SELECT FALSE, 'Seats no longer available (taken by another booking)'::TEXT, NULL::UUID;
        RETURN;
    END IF;
    
    -- Create booking record
    INSERT INTO bookings (
        quote_id,
        flight_id,
        seats_booked,
        client_name,
        client_phone
    ) VALUES (
        p_quote_id,
        p_flight_id,
        p_seats_requested,
        p_client_name,
        p_client_phone
    )
    RETURNING id INTO v_booking_id;
    
    -- Update quote status if provided
    IF p_quote_id IS NOT NULL THEN
        UPDATE quotes
        SET status = 'accepted', updated_at = NOW()
        WHERE id = p_quote_id;
    END IF;
    
    RETURN QUERY SELECT TRUE, 'Booking successful'::TEXT, v_booking_id;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- CANCEL_BOOKING FUNCTION
-- Restores seats when a booking is cancelled
-- ============================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION cancel_booking(
    p_booking_id UUID
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    success BOOLEAN,
    message TEXT
) AS $$
DECLARE
    v_flight_id UUID;
    v_seats INTEGER;
BEGIN
    -- Get booking details
    SELECT flight_id, seats_booked 
    INTO v_flight_id, v_seats
    FROM bookings
    WHERE id = p_booking_id;
    
    IF v_flight_id IS NULL THEN
        RETURN QUERY SELECT FALSE, 'Booking not found'::TEXT;
        RETURN;
    END IF;
    
    -- Restore seats
    UPDATE flights
    SET 
        available_seats = available_seats + v_seats,
        updated_at = NOW()
    WHERE id = v_flight_id;
    
    -- Delete booking
    DELETE FROM bookings WHERE id = p_booking_id;
    
    RETURN QUERY SELECT TRUE, format('Booking cancelled, %s seats restored', v_seats)::TEXT;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- ============================================
-- CHECK_AVAILABILITY FUNCTION
-- Quick check without locking
-- ============================================
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION check_availability(
    p_flight_id UUID,
    p_seats_needed INTEGER DEFAULT 1
)
RETURNS TABLE (
    is_available BOOLEAN,
    available_seats INTEGER,
    flight_info JSONB
) AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT 
        (f.available_seats >= p_seats_needed) AS is_available,
        f.available_seats,
        jsonb_build_object(
            'id', f.id,
            'airline', f.airline,
            'destination', f.destination_name,
            'departure_date', f.departure_date,
            'selling_price', f.selling_price
        ) AS flight_info
    FROM flights f
    WHERE f.id = p_flight_id;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

-- Migration 004: RLS for secure data access

-- ============================================
-- ENABLE RLS ON ALL TABLES
-- ============================================
ALTER TABLE flights ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE quotes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE quote_flights ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE bookings ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE audit_log ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- ============================================
-- FLIGHTS POLICIES
-- Authenticated users: Full access
-- Anonymous: No direct access (use public_inventory view via Edge Function)
-- ============================================

-- Authenticated users can view all flights
CREATE POLICY "Authenticated users can view flights"
ON flights FOR SELECT
TO authenticated
USING (true);

-- Authenticated users can insert flights
CREATE POLICY "Authenticated users can insert flights"
ON flights FOR INSERT
TO authenticated
WITH CHECK (true);

-- Authenticated users can update flights
CREATE POLICY "Authenticated users can update flights"
ON flights FOR UPDATE
TO authenticated
USING (true)
WITH CHECK (true);

-- Authenticated users can delete flights
CREATE POLICY "Authenticated users can delete flights"
ON flights FOR DELETE
TO authenticated
USING (true);

import type { Database } from '../types/database'

// Get environment variables
const supabaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL || ''
const supabaseAnonKey = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || ''

if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseAnonKey) {
  console.warn('Supabase credentials not configured. Set VITE_SUPABASE_URL and VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY in .env')
}

export const supabase = createClient<Database>(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey)

// Helper to check connection
export async function checkConnection(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    const { error } = await supabase.from('flights').select('id').limit(1)
    return !error
  } catch {
    return false
  }
}

// Typed helpers for common operations
export const db = {
  flights: {
    async getAll() {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('flights')
        .select('*')
        .order('departure_date', { ascending: true })
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async getAvailable() {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('flights')
        .select('*')
        .gt('available_seats', 0)
        .order('departure_date', { ascending: true })
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async getById(id: string) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('flights')
        .select('*')
        .eq('id', id)
        .single()
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async create(flight: Omit<Database['public']['Tables']['flights']['Insert'], 'id'>) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('flights')
        .insert(flight)
        .select()
        .single()
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async update(id: string, updates: Partial<Database['public']['Tables']['flights']['Update']>) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('flights')
        .update(updates)
        .eq('id', id)
        .select()
        .single()
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async delete(id: string) {
      const { error } = await supabase
        .from('flights')
        .delete()
        .eq('id', id)
      if (error) throw error
    }
  },

  quotes: {
    async getAll() {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('quotes')
        .select('*')
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async getById(id: string) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('quotes')
        .select(`
          *,
          quote_flights (
            *,
            flight:flights (*)
          )
        `)
        .eq('id', id)
        .single()
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async create(quote: Omit<Database['public']['Tables']['quotes']['Insert'], 'id'>) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('quotes')
        .insert(quote)
        .select()
        .single()
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    },

    async update(id: string, updates: Partial<Database['public']['Tables']['quotes']['Update']>) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('quotes')
        .update(updates)
        .eq('id', id)
        .select()
        .single()
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    }
  },

  bookings: {
    async bookSeats(flightId: string, seatsRequested: number, quoteId?: string, clientName?: string) {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .rpc('book_seats', {
          p_flight_id: flightId,
          p_seats_requested: seatsRequested,
          p_quote_id: quoteId || null,
          p_client_name: clientName || 'Guest'
        })
      if (error) throw error
      return data
    }
  }
}

// Real-time subscription helper
export function subscribeToFlights(callback: (payload: any) => void) {
  return supabase
    .channel('flights-changes')
    .on(
      'postgres_changes',
      { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'flights' },
      callback
    )
    .subscribe()
}

  path: c:\Users\shake\lord-tickets-app\web\src\stores\quoteStore.ts
  contents: import { create } from 'zustand'
import { persist } from 'zustand/middleware'
import type { Flight, Quote } from '../types/database'

// Quote draft - unsaved quote being worked on
interface QuoteDraft {
  id?: string
  clientName: string
  clientPhone: string
  clientEmail: string
  flights: Array<{
    flight: Flight
    seats: number
    priceOverride?: number
  }>
  hotels: Array<{
    name: string
    checkIn: string
    checkOut: string
    rooms: number
    pricePerNight: number
  }>
  services: Array<{
    description: string
    price: number
  }>
  markupPercent: number
  notes: string
}

interface QuoteState {
  // Active quote being edited
  activeQuoteId: string | null
  draft: QuoteDraft | null
  
  // Quote tabs (multiple quotes open)
  openQuotes: Array<{
    id: string
    name: string
    isDirty: boolean
  }>
  
  // Actions
  setActiveQuote: (id: string | null) => void
  createNewQuote: () => void
  updateDraft: (updates: Partial<QuoteDraft>) => void
  
  // Flight management
  addFlightToQuote: (flight: Flight, seats?: number) => void
  removeFlightFromQuote: (flightId: string) => void
  updateFlightSeats: (flightId: string, seats: number) => void
  
  // Hotel management
  addHotelToQuote: (hotel: QuoteDraft['hotels'][0]) => void
  removeHotelFromQuote: (index: number) => void
  
  // Service management
  addServiceToQuote: (service: QuoteDraft['services'][0]) => void
  removeServiceFromQuote: (index: number) => void
  
  // Calculations
  getTotalCost: () => number
  getTotalPrice: () => number
  getProfit: () => number
  
  // Reset
  clearDraft: () => void
  closeQuote: (id: string) => void
}

const createEmptyDraft = (): QuoteDraft => ({
  clientName: '',
  clientPhone: '',
  clientEmail: '',
  flights: [],
  hotels: [],
  services: [],
  markupPercent: 15,
  notes: ''
})

export const useQuoteStore = create<QuoteState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      activeQuoteId: null,
      draft: createEmptyDraft(),
      openQuotes: [],
      
      setActiveQuote: (id) => set({ activeQuoteId: id }),
      
      createNewQuote: () => {
        const newId = `new-${Date.now()}`
        set(state => ({
          activeQuoteId: newId,
          draft: createEmptyDraft(),
          openQuotes: [
            ...state.openQuotes,
            { id: newId, name: 'הצעה חדשה', isDirty: false }
          ]
        }))
      },
      
      updateDraft: (updates) => set(state => ({
        draft: state.draft ? { ...state.draft, ...updates } : createEmptyDraft(),
        openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
          q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
        )
      })),
      
      addFlightToQuote: (flight, seats = 1) => set(state => {
        if (!state.draft) return state
        
        // Check if flight already in quote
        const existingIndex = state.draft.flights.findIndex(f => f.flight.id === flight.id)
        if (existingIndex >= 0) {
          // Update seats instead
          const newFlights = [...state.draft.flights]
          newFlights[existingIndex].seats += seats
          return {
            draft: { ...state.draft, flights: newFlights },
            openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
              q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
            )
          }
        }
        
        return {
          draft: {
            ...state.draft,
            flights: [...state.draft.flights, { flight, seats }]
          },
          openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
            q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
          )
        }
      }),
      
      removeFlightFromQuote: (flightId) => set(state => {
        if (!state.draft) return state
        return {
          draft: {
            ...state.draft,
            flights: state.draft.flights.filter(f => f.flight.id !== flightId)
          },
          openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
            q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
          )
        }
      }),
      
      updateFlightSeats: (flightId, seats) => set(state => {
        if (!state.draft) return state
        return {
          draft: {
            ...state.draft,
            flights: state.draft.flights.map(f => 
              f.flight.id === flightId ? { ...f, seats } : f
            )
          },
          openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
            q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
          )
        }
      }),
      
      addHotelToQuote: (hotel) => set(state => {
        if (!state.draft) return state
        return {
          draft: {
            ...state.draft,
            hotels: [...state.draft.hotels, hotel]
          },
          openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
            q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
          )
        }
      }),
      
      removeHotelFromQuote: (index) => set(state => {
        if (!state.draft) return state
        return {
          draft: {
            ...state.draft,
            hotels: state.draft.hotels.filter((_, i) => i !== index)
          },
          openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
            q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
          )
        }
      }),
      
      addServiceToQuote: (service) => set(state => {
        if (!state.draft) return state
        return {
          draft: {
            ...state.draft,
            services: [...state.draft.services, service]
          },
          openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
            q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
          )
        }
      }),
      
      removeServiceFromQuote: (index) => set(state => {
        if (!state.draft) return state
        return {
          draft: {
            ...state.draft,
            services: state.draft.services.filter((_, i) => i !== index)
          },
          openQuotes: state.openQuotes.map(q => 
            q.id === state.activeQuoteId ? { ...q, isDirty: true } : q
          )
        }
      }),
      
      getTotalCost: () => {
        const { draft } = get()
        if (!draft) return 0
        
        const flightsCost = draft.flights.reduce((sum, f) => 
          sum + (f.flight.cost_price || 0) * f.seats, 0)
        const hotelsCost = draft.hotels.reduce((sum, h) => {
          const nights = Math.ceil(
            (new Date(h.checkOut).getTime() - new Date(h.checkIn).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)
          )
          return sum + h.pricePerNight * h.rooms * nights
        }, 0)
        const servicesCost = draft.services.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.price, 0)
        
        return flightsCost + hotelsCost + servicesCost
      },
      
      getTotalPrice: () => {
        const { draft, getTotalCost } = get()
        if (!draft) return 0
        
        const cost = getTotalCost()
        const markup = 1 + (draft.markupPercent / 100)
        
        // Use flight selling prices if available
        const flightsPrice = draft.flights.reduce((sum, f) => 
          sum + (f.priceOverride || f.flight.selling_price) * f.seats, 0)
        const hotelsCost = draft.hotels.reduce((sum, h) => {
          const nights = Math.ceil(
            (new Date(h.checkOut).getTime() - new Date(h.checkIn).getTime()) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)
          )
          return sum + h.pricePerNight * h.rooms * nights
        }, 0)
        const servicesCost = draft.services.reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.price, 0)
        
        return flightsPrice + (hotelsCost + servicesCost) * markup
      },
      
      getProfit: () => {
        const { getTotalPrice, getTotalCost } = get()
        return getTotalPrice() - getTotalCost()
      },
      
      clearDraft: () => set({ draft: createEmptyDraft() }),
      
      closeQuote: (id) => set(state => ({
        openQuotes: state.openQuotes.filter(q => q.id !== id),
        activeQuoteId: state.activeQuoteId === id 
          ? (state.openQuotes.find(q => q.id !== id)?.id || null)
          : state.activeQuoteId
      }))
    }),
    {
      name: 'lord-tickets-quotes',
      partialize: (state) => ({
        draft: state.draft,
        openQuotes: state.openQuotes,
        activeQuoteId: state.activeQuoteId
      })
    }
  )
)

[Tool call] Write
  path: c:\Users\shake\lord-tickets-app\web\src\stores\inventoryStore.ts
  contents: import { create } from 'zustand'
import type { Flight } from '../types/database'
import { db, subscribeToFlights, supabase } from '../lib/supabase'

interface InventoryState {
  flights: Flight[]
  isLoading: boolean
  error: string | null
  
  // Filters
  searchQuery: string
  airlineFilter: string | null
  destinationFilter: string | null
  dateRange: { start: string | null; end: string | null }
  showOnlyAvailable: boolean
  
  // Actions
  fetchFlights: () => Promise<void>
  setSearchQuery: (query: string) => void
  setAirlineFilter: (airline: string | null) => void
  setDestinationFilter: (destination: string | null) => void
  setDateRange: (range: { start: string | null; end: string | null }) => void
  setShowOnlyAvailable: (show: boolean) => void
  
  // CRUD
  addFlight: (flight: Omit<Flight, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'>) => Promise<Flight>
  updateFlight: (id: string, updates: Partial<Flight>) => Promise<Flight>
  deleteFlight: (id: string) => Promise<void>
  
  // Real-time
  subscribeToChanges: () => () => void
  
  // Computed
  getFilteredFlights: () => Flight[]
  getUniqueAirlines: () => string[]
  getUniqueDestinations: () => string[]
}

export const useInventoryStore = create<InventoryState>((set, get) => ({
  flights: [],
  isLoading: false,
  error: null,
  
  searchQuery: '',
  airlineFilter: null,
  destinationFilter: null,
  dateRange: { start: null, end: null },
  showOnlyAvailable: true,
  
  fetchFlights: async () => {
    set({ isLoading: true, error: null })
    try {
      const flights = await db.flights.getAll()
      set({ flights, isLoading: false })
    } catch (error) {
      set({ 
        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to fetch flights',
        isLoading: false 
      })
    }
  },
  
  setSearchQuery: (query) => set({ searchQuery: query }),
  setAirlineFilter: (airline) => set({ airlineFilter: airline }),
  setDestinationFilter: (destination) => set({ destinationFilter: destination }),
  setDateRange: (range) => set({ dateRange: range }),
  setShowOnlyAvailable: (show) => set({ showOnlyAvailable: show }),
  
  addFlight: async (flightData) => {
    const flight = await db.flights.create(flightData)
    set(state => ({ flights: [...state.flights, flight] }))
    return flight
  },
  
  updateFlight: async (id, updates) => {
    const flight = await db.flights.update(id, updates)
    set(state => ({
      flights: state.flights.map(f => f.id === id ? flight : f)
    }))
    return flight
  },
  
  deleteFlight: async (id) => {
    await db.flights.delete(id)
    set(state => ({
      flights: state.flights.filter(f => f.id !== id)
    }))
  },
  
  subscribeToChanges: () => {
    const channel = subscribeToFlights((payload) => {
      const { eventType, new: newRecord, old: oldRecord } = payload
      
      set(state => {
        switch (eventType) {
          case 'INSERT':
            return { flights: [...state.flights, newRecord as Flight] }
          case 'UPDATE':
            return {
              flights: state.flights.map(f => 
                f.id === (newRecord as Flight).id ? newRecord as Flight : f
              )
            }
          case 'DELETE':
            return {
              flights: state.flights.filter(f => f.id !== (oldRecord as Flight).id)
            }
          default:
            return state
        }
      })
    })
    
    // Return unsubscribe function
    return () => {
      supabase.removeChannel(channel)
    }
  },
  
  getFilteredFlights: () => {
    const { 
      flights, 
      searchQuery, 
      airlineFilter, 
      destinationFilter, 
      dateRange,
      showOnlyAvailable 
    } = get()
    
    return flights.filter(flight => {
      // Available seats filter
      if (showOnlyAvailable && flight.available_seats <= 0) {
        return false
      }
      
      // Search query
      if (searchQuery) {
        const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase()
        const searchFields = [
          flight.airline,
          flight.destination_name,
          flight.destination_code,
          flight.outbound_details,
          flight.inbound_details
        ].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase()
        
        if (!searchFields.includes(query)) {
          return false
        }
      }
      
      // Airline filter
      if (airlineFilter && flight.airline !== airlineFilter) {
        return false
      }
      
      // Destination filter
      if (destinationFilter && flight.destination_code !== destinationFilter) {
        return false
      }
      
      // Date range filter
      if (dateRange.start && flight.departure_date < dateRange.start) {
        return false
      }
      if (dateRange.end && flight.departure_date > dateRange.end) {
        return false
      }
      
      return true
    })
  },
  
  getUniqueAirlines: () => {
    const { flights } = get()
    return [...new Set(flights.map(f => f.airline))].sort()
  },
  
  getUniqueDestinations: () => {
    const { flights } = get()
    return [...new Set(flights.map(f => f.destination_code))].sort()
  }
}))

export default function QuoteBuilder() {
  const { 
    draft, 
    updateDraft, 
    removeFlightFromQuote,
    getTotalCost,
    getTotalPrice,
    getProfit
  } = useQuoteStore()

  if (!draft) {
    return (
      <div className="empty-state">
        <h2>אין הצעות פתוחות</h2>
        <p>לחץ על "+ חדש" ליצירת הצעת מחיר חדשה</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="quote-builder">
      {/* Client Info */}
      <section className="card">
        <div className="card-header">פרטי לקוח</div>
        <div className="card-body">
          <div className="form-grid">
            <div className="form-group">
              <label>שם לקוח</label>
              <input
                type="text"
                className="input"
                value={draft.clientName}
                onChange={(e) => updateDraft({ clientName: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="שם מלא"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label>טלפון</label>
              <input
                type="tel"
                className="input"
                value={draft.clientPhone}
                onChange={(e) => updateDraft({ clientPhone: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="050-0000000"
                dir="ltr"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="form-group">
              <label>אימייל</label>
              <input
                type="email"
                className="input"
                value={draft.clientEmail}
                onChange={(e) => updateDraft({ clientEmail: e.target.value })}
                placeholder="email@example.com"
                dir="ltr"
              />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Flights */}
      <section className="card">
        <div className="card-header">
          <span>טיסות</span>
          <button className="btn btn-outline btn-sm">+ הוסף טיסה</button>
        </div>
        <div className="card-body">
          {draft.flights.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="empty-section">
              <p>אין טיסות בהצעה</p>
              <p className="hint">לחץ על "מלאי" להוספת טיסות</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flights-list">
              {draft.flights.map((item, index) => (
                <div key={item.flight.id} className="flight-item">
                  <div className="flight-info">
                    <strong>{item.flight.destination_name}</strong>
                    <span>{item.flight.airline}</span>
                    <span>{item.flight.departure_date}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flight-details">
                    <span>{item.flight.outbound_details}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flight-pricing">
                    <span>{item.seats} מושבים</span>
                    <span>${item.flight.selling_price * item.seats}</span>
                  </div>
                  <button 
                    className="btn btn-ghost"
                    onClick={() => removeFlightFromQuote(item.flight.id)}
                  >
                    🗑️
                  </button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* Pricing Summary */}
      <section className="card pricing-card">
        <div className="card-header">סיכום מחירים</div>
        <div className="card-body">
          <div className="pricing-row">
            <span>עלות:</span>
            <span>${getTotalCost().toFixed(2)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="pricing-row">
            <span>מרווח ({draft.markupPercent}%):</span>

              type="number"
              className="input markup-input"
              value={draft.markupPercent}
              onChange={(e) => updateDraft({ markupPercent: Number(e.target.value) })}
              min="0"
              max="100"
            />
          </div>
          <div className="pricing-row total">

        </div>
