import { AgGridReact } from 'ag-grid-react'
import { ColDef, GridReadyEvent, CellValueChangedEvent } from 'ag-grid-community'
import 'ag-grid-community/styles/ag-grid.css'
import 'ag-grid-community/styles/ag-theme-alpine.css'
import { useInventoryStore } from '../../stores/inventoryStore'
import type { Flight } from '../../types/database'
import { format } from 'date-fns'

interface InventoryPanelProps {
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  onSelectFlight: (flight: Flight) => void
}

export default function InventoryPanel({ open, onClose, onSelectFlight }: InventoryPanelProps) {
  const {
    isLoading,
    error,
    searchQuery,
    airlineFilter,
    showOnlyAvailable,
    fetchFlights,
    setSearchQuery,
    setAirlineFilter,
    setShowOnlyAvailable,
    updateFlight,
    getFilteredFlights,
    getUniqueAirlines,
    subscribeToChanges
  } = useInventoryStore()

  const [selectedFlight, setSelectedFlight] = useState<Flight | null>(null)

  // Fetch flights and subscribe on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchFlights()
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToChanges()
    return unsubscribe
  }, [])

  const flights = getFilteredFlights()
  const airlines = getUniqueAirlines()

  // AG Grid column definitions
  const columnDefs = useMemo<ColDef<Flight>[]>(() => [
    {
      headerName: 'תאריכים',
      field: 'departure_date',
      width: 120,
      valueFormatter: (params) => {
        if (!params.value) return ''
        return format(new Date(params.value), 'dd/MM/yy')
      }
    },
    {
      headerName: 'יעד',
      field: 'destination_name',
      width: 130,
      cellRenderer: (params: any) => (
        <span>
          {params.value} <small>({params.data.destination_code})</small>
        </span>
      )
    },
    {
      headerName: 'חברה',
      field: 'airline',
      width: 100
    },
    {
      headerName: 'הלוך',
      field: 'outbound_details',
      width: 200,
      tooltipField: 'outbound_details'
    },
    {
      headerName: 'חזור',
      field: 'inbound_details',
      width: 200,
      tooltipField: 'inbound_details'
    },
    {
      headerName: 'מושבים',
      field: 'available_seats',
      width: 90,
      editable: true,
      cellStyle: (params) => ({
        backgroundColor: params.value <= 2 ? '#fff3cd' : params.value === 0 ? '#f8d7da' : 'inherit',
        fontWeight: params.value <= 2 ? 'bold' : 'normal'
      })
    },
    {
      headerName: 'מחיר',
      field: 'selling_price',
      width: 100,
      editable: true,
      valueFormatter: (params) => params.value ? `$${params.value}` : ''
    },
    {
      headerName: 'עלות',
      field: 'cost_price',
      width: 100,
      editable: true,
      valueFormatter: (params) => params.value ? `$${params.value}` : '-',
      cellStyle: { color: '#666' }
    },
    {
      headerName: '',
      width: 80,
      cellRenderer: (params: any) => (
        <button
          className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
          onClick={() => onSelectFlight(params.data)}
        >
          + הוסף
        </button>
      )
    }
  ], [onSelectFlight])

  // Default column settings
  const defaultColDef = useMemo<ColDef>(() => ({
    sortable: true,
    filter: true,
    resizable: true
  }), [])

  // Handle cell edit
  const onCellValueChanged = useCallback(async (event: CellValueChangedEvent<Flight>) => {
    if (event.data && event.colDef.field) {
      try {
        await updateFlight(event.data.id, {
          [event.colDef.field]: event.newValue
        })
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to update:', err)
        // Revert the change
        event.node.setDataValue(event.colDef.field, event.oldValue)
      }
    }
  }, [updateFlight])

  return (
    <>
      {/* Overlay */}
      <div 
        className={`panel-overlay ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}
        onClick={onClose}
      />
      
      {/* Panel */}
      <div className={`panel ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}>
        <div className="panel-header">
          <h2 className="panel-title">📦 מלאי טיסות</h2>
          <button className="panel-close" onClick={onClose}>×</button>
        </div>
        
        {/* Filters */}
        <div className="panel-filters">
          <input
            type="text"
            className="input search-input"
            placeholder="חיפוש..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
          
          <select
            className="input filter-select"
            value={airlineFilter || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setAirlineFilter(e.target.value || null)}
          >
            <option value="">כל החברות</option>
            {airlines.map(airline => (
              <option key={airline} value={airline}>{airline}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          
          <label className="checkbox-label">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={showOnlyAvailable}
              onChange={(e) => setShowOnlyAvailable(e.target.checked)}
            />
            רק זמינים
          </label>
        </div>
        
        {/* Grid */}
        <div className="panel-body">
          {error && (
            <div className="error-banner">
              ❌ {error}
              <button onClick={fetchFlights}>נסה שוב</button>
            </div>
          )}
          
          <div 
            className="ag-theme-alpine" 
            style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }}
          >
            <AgGridReact<Flight>
              rowData={flights}
              columnDefs={columnDefs}
              defaultColDef={defaultColDef}
              onCellValueChanged={onCellValueChanged}
              rowSelection="single"
              animateRows={true}
              enableRtl={true}
              loading={isLoading}
              overlayLoadingTemplate='<span class="ag-overlay-loading-center">טוען...</span>'
              overlayNoRowsTemplate='<span class="ag-overlay-no-rows-center">אין טיסות להצגה</span>'
            />
          </div>
        </div>
        
        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="panel-footer">
          <span>{flights.length} טיסות</span>
          <div className="panel-footer-actions">
            <button className="btn btn-outline">📥 יבוא CSV</button>
            <button className="btn btn-outline">📤 ייצוא</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <style>{`
        .panel-filters {
          display: flex;
          gap: var(--space-3);
          padding: var(--space-4) var(--space-6);
          border-bottom: 1px solid var(--gray-200);
          flex-wrap: wrap;
        }
        
        .search-input {
          flex: 1;
          min-width: 200px;
        }
        
        .filter-select {
          width: 150px;
        }
        
        .checkbox-label {
          display: flex;
          align-items: center;
          gap: var(--space-2);
          cursor: pointer;
          font-size: var(--font-size-sm);
        }
        
        .error-banner {
          background: var(--error-light);
          color: var(--error);
          padding: var(--space-3);
          margin-bottom: var(--space-3);
          border-radius: var(--radius-md);
          display: flex;
          justify-content: space-between;
          align-items: center;
        }
        
        .error-banner button {
          background: var(--error);
          color: white;
          border: none;

  path: c:\Users\shake\lord-tickets-app\web\README.md
  contents: # Lord Tickets - Web Application

Modern React + TypeScript web application for managing flight inventory and generating customer quotes.

## Tech Stack

- **Frontend**: React 18 + Vite + TypeScript
- **UI Grid**: AG Grid Community (Excel-like editing)
- **State**: Zustand (with localStorage persistence)
- **Backend**: Supabase (PostgreSQL + Realtime + Edge Functions)
- **Styling**: CSS Variables with Gold/Navy theme

## Prerequisites

- Node.js 18+ installed
- Supabase project (free tier works)

## Setup

### 1. Install Dependencies

```bash
cd web
npm install
```

### 2. Configure Supabase

1. Create a new project at [supabase.com](https://supabase.com)
2. Run the migrations in `../supabase/migrations/` in order:
   - `001_create_tables.sql`
   - `002_public_view.sql`
   - `003_booking_function.sql`
   - `004_rls_policies.sql`
3. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and fill in your Supabase credentials:

```bash
cp .env.example .env
```

Edit `.env`:
```
VITE_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key-here
```

### 3. Run Development Server

```bash
npm run dev
```

Open http://localhost:5173

## Project Structure

```
web/
├── src/
│   ├── components/
│   │   ├── layout/          # Header, TabsBar, Footer
│   │   ├── QuoteBuilder/    # Quote creation UI
│   │   └── InventoryGrid/   # AG Grid inventory panel
│   ├── hooks/               # Custom React hooks
│   ├── stores/              # Zustand state stores
│   │   ├── quoteStore.ts    # Quote/draft management
│   │   └── inventoryStore.ts # Flight inventory
│   ├── lib/
│   │   └── supabase.ts      # Supabase client & helpers
│   ├── types/
│   │   └── database.ts      # TypeScript types for DB
│   └── styles/
│       ├── variables.css    # Design system tokens
│       └── main.css         # Global styles
├── supabase/
│   └── migrations/          # SQL schema files
└── package.json
```

## Features

### Inventory Management
- Excel-like inline editing with AG Grid
- Real-time sync across multiple users
- Filter by airline, destination, date
- Search functionality

### Quote Builder
- Multi-tab support for multiple quotes
- Add flights from inventory
- Automatic pricing calculations
- PDF generation (coming soon)
- WhatsApp sharing (coming soon)

### Security
- Row Level Security (RLS) for authenticated access
- Public inventory view excludes cost prices
- Edge Function API gateway for public access

## Scripts

- `npm run dev` - Start development server
- `npm run build` - Build for production
- `npm run preview` - Preview production build
- `npm run lint` - Run ESLint

## Deployment

Build the production bundle:

```bash
npm run build
```

Deploy the `dist/` folder to any static hosting (Vercel, Netlify, GitHub Pages).

## License

Private - Lord Tickets

## New Project Structure

## Next Steps

To run the application, you'll need to:

1. **Install Node.js** from [nodejs.org](https://nodejs.org) (LTS version recommended)

2. **Install dependencies**:
   ```bash
   cd lord-tickets-app/web
   npm install
   ```

3. **Set up Supabase**:

   - Run the 4 migration files in the SQL editor

   ```bash

+import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
+import type { Database } from '../types/database'
+
+// Get environment variables
+const supabaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL || ''
+const supabaseAnonKey = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || ''
+
+if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseAnonKey) {
+  console.warn('Supabase credentials not configured. Set VITE_SUPABASE_URL and VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY in .env')
+}
+
+export const supabase = createClient<Database>(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey)
+
+// Helper to check connection
+export async function checkConnection(): Promise<boolean> {
+  try {
+    const { error } = await supabase.from('flights').select('id').limit(1)
+    return !error
+  } catch {
+    return false
+  }
+}
+
+// Typed helpers for common operations
+export const db = {
+  flights: {
+    async getAll() {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('flights')
+        .select('*')
+        .order('departure_date', { ascending: true })
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async getAvailable() {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('flights')
+        .select('*')
+        .gt('available_seats', 0)
+        .order('departure_date', { ascending: true })
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async getById(id: string) {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('flights')
+        .select('*')
+        .eq('id', id)
+        .single()
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async create(flight: Omit<Database['public']['Tables']['flights']['Insert'], 'id'>) {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('flights')
+        .insert(flight)
+        .select()
+        .single()
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async update(id: string, updates: Partial<Database['public']['Tables']['flights']['Update']>) {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('flights')
+        .update(updates)
+        .eq('id', id)
+        .select()
+        .single()
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async delete(id: string) {
+      const { error } = await supabase
+        .from('flights')
+        .delete()
+        .eq('id', id)
+      if (error) throw error
+    }
+  },
+
+  quotes: {
+    async getAll() {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('quotes')
+        .select('*')
+        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async getById(id: string) {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('quotes')
+        .select(`
+          *,
+          quote_flights (
+            *,
+            flight:flights (*)
+          )
+        `)
+        .eq('id', id)
+        .single()
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async create(quote: Omit<Database['public']['Tables']['quotes']['Insert'], 'id'>) {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('quotes')
+        .insert(quote)
+        .select()
+        .single()
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    },
+
+    async update(id: string, updates: Partial<Database['public']['Tables']['quotes']['Update']>) {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .from('quotes')
+        .update(updates)
+        .eq('id', id)
+        .select()
+        .single()
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    }
+  },
+
+  bookings: {
+    async bookSeats(flightId: string, seatsRequested: number, quoteId?: string, clientName?: string) {
+      const { data, error } = await supabase
+        .rpc('book_seats', {
+          p_flight_id: flightId,
+          p_seats_requested: seatsRequested,
+          p_quote_id: quoteId || null,
+          p_client_name: clientName || 'Guest'
+        })
+      if (error) throw error
+      return data
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+// Real-time subscription helper
+export function subscribeToFlights(callback: (payload: any) => void) {
+  return supabase
+    .channel('flights-changes')
+    .on(
+      'postgres_changes',
+      { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'flights' },
+      callback
+    )
+    .subscribe()
+}

+# Lord Tickets - Web Application
+
+Modern React + TypeScript web application for managing flight inventory and generating customer quotes.
+
+## Tech Stack
+
+- **Frontend**: React 18 + Vite + TypeScript
+- **UI Grid**: AG Grid Community (Excel-like editing)
+- **State**: Zustand (with localStorage persistence)
+- **Backend**: Supabase (PostgreSQL + Realtime + Edge Functions)
+- **Styling**: CSS Variables with Gold/Navy theme
+
+## Prerequisites
+
+- Node.js 18+ installed
+- Supabase project (free tier works)
+
+## Setup
+
+### 1. Install Dependencies
+
+```bash
+cd web
+npm install
+```
+
+### 2. Configure Supabase
+
+1. Create a new project at [supabase.com](https://supabase.com)
+2. Run the migrations in `../supabase/migrations/` in order:
+   - `001_create_tables.sql`
+   - `002_public_view.sql`
+   - `003_booking_function.sql`
+   - `004_rls_policies.sql`
+3. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and fill in your Supabase credentials:
+
+```bash
+cp .env.example .env
+```
+
+Edit `.env`:
+```
+VITE_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
+VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key-here
+```
+
+### 3. Run Development Server
+
+```bash
+npm run dev
+```
+
+Open http://localhost:5173
+
+## Project Structure
+
+```
+web/
+├── src/
+│   ├── components/
+│   │   ├── layout/          # Header, TabsBar, Footer
+│   │   ├── QuoteBuilder/    # Quote creation UI
+│   │   └── InventoryGrid/   # AG Grid inventory panel
+│   ├── hooks/               # Custom React hooks
+│   ├── stores/              # Zustand state stores
+│   │   ├── quoteStore.ts    # Quote/draft management
+│   │   └── inventoryStore.ts # Flight inventory
+│   ├── lib/
+│   │   └── supabase.ts      # Supabase client & helpers
+│   ├── types/
+│   │   └── database.ts      # TypeScript types for DB
+│   └── styles/
+│       ├── variables.css    # Design system tokens
+│       └── main.css         # Global styles
+├── supabase/
+│   └── migrations/          # SQL schema files
+└── package.json
+```
+
+## Features
+
+### Inventory Management
+- Excel-like inline editing with AG Grid
+- Real-time sync across multiple users
+- Filter by airline, destination, date
+- Search functionality
+
+### Quote Builder
+- Multi-tab support for multiple quotes
+- Add flights from inventory
+- Automatic pricing calculations
+- PDF generation (coming soon)
+- WhatsApp sharing (coming soon)
+
+### Security
+- Row Level Security (RLS) for authenticated access
+- Public inventory view excludes cost prices
+- Edge Function API gateway for public access
+
+## Scripts
+
+- `npm run dev` - Start development server
+- `npm run build` - Build for production
+- `npm run preview` - Preview production build
+- `npm run lint` - Run ESLint
+
+## Deployment
+
+Build the production bundle:
+
+```bash
+npm run build
+```
+
+Deploy the `dist/` folder to any static hosting (Vercel, Netlify, GitHub Pages).
+
+## License
+
+Private - Lord Tickets

import { useInventoryStore } from '../../stores/inventoryStore'
import type { Flight } from '../../types/database'
import { format } from 'date-fns'

interface InventoryPanelProps {
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  onSelectFlight: (flight: Flight) => void
}

export default function InventoryPanel({ open, onClose, onSelectFlight }: InventoryPanelProps) {
  const {
    isLoading,
    error,
    searchQuery,
    airlineFilter,
    showOnlyAvailable,
    fetchFlights,
    setSearchQuery,
    setAirlineFilter,
    setShowOnlyAvailable,
    updateFlight,
    getFilteredFlights,
    getUniqueAirlines,
    subscribeToChanges
  } = useInventoryStore()

  const [selectedFlight, setSelectedFlight] = useState<Flight | null>(null)

  // Fetch flights and subscribe on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchFlights()
    const unsubscribe = subscribeToChanges()
    return unsubscribe
  }, [])

  const flights = getFilteredFlights()
  const airlines = getUniqueAirlines()

  // AG Grid column definitions
  const columnDefs = useMemo<ColDef<Flight>[]>(() => [
    {
      headerName: 'תאריכים',
      field: 'departure_date',
      width: 120,
      valueFormatter: (params) => {
        if (!params.value) return ''
        return format(new Date(params.value), 'dd/MM/yy')
      }
    },
    {
      headerName: 'יעד',
      field: 'destination_name',
      width: 130,
      cellRenderer: (params: any) => (
        <span>
          {params.value} <small>({params.data.destination_code})</small>
        </span>
      )
    },
    {
      headerName: 'חברה',
      field: 'airline',
      width: 100
    },
    {
      headerName: 'הלוך',
      field: 'outbound_details',
      width: 200,
      tooltipField: 'outbound_details'
    },
    {
      headerName: 'חזור',
      field: 'inbound_details',
      width: 200,
      tooltipField: 'inbound_details'
    },
    {
      headerName: 'מושבים',
      field: 'available_seats',
      width: 90,
      editable: true,
      cellStyle: (params) => ({
        backgroundColor: params.value <= 2 ? '#fff3cd' : params.value === 0 ? '#f8d7da' : 'inherit',
        fontWeight: params.value <= 2 ? 'bold' : 'normal'
      })
    },
    {
      headerName: 'מחיר',
      field: 'selling_price',
      width: 100,
      editable: true,
      valueFormatter: (params) => params.value ? `$${params.value}` : ''
    },
    {
      headerName: 'עלות',
      field: 'cost_price',
      width: 100,
      editable: true,
      valueFormatter: (params) => params.value ? `$${params.value}` : '-',
      cellStyle: { color: '#666' }
    },
    {
      headerName: '',
      width: 80,
      cellRenderer: (params: any) => (
        <button
          className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
          onClick={() => onSelectFlight(params.data)}
        >
          + הוסף
        </button>
      )
    }
  ], [onSelectFlight])

  // Default column settings
  const defaultColDef = useMemo<ColDef>(() => ({
    sortable: true,
    filter: true,
    resizable: true
  }), [])

  // Handle cell edit
  const onCellValueChanged = useCallback(async (event: CellValueChangedEvent<Flight>) => {
    if (event.data && event.colDef.field) {
      try {
        await updateFlight(event.data.id, {
          [event.colDef.field]: event.newValue
        })
      } catch (err) {
        console.error('Failed to update:', err)
        // Revert the change
        event.node.setDataValue(event.colDef.field, event.oldValue)
      }
    }
  }, [updateFlight])

  return (
    <>
      {/* Overlay */}
      <div 
        className={`panel-overlay ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}
        onClick={onClose}
      />
      
      {/* Panel */}
      <div className={`panel ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}>
        <div className="panel-header">
          <h2 className="panel-title">📦 מלאי טיסות</h2>
          <button className="panel-close" onClick={onClose}>×</button>
        </div>
        
        {/* Filters */}
        <div className="panel-filters">
          <input
            type="text"
            className="input search-input"
            placeholder="חיפוש..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
          
          <select
            className="input filter-select"
            value={airlineFilter || ''}
            onChange={(e) => setAirlineFilter(e.target.value || null)}
          >
            <option value="">כל החברות</option>
            {airlines.map(airline => (
              <option key={airline} value={airline}>{airline}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
          
          <label className="checkbox-label">
            <input
              type="checkbox"
              checked={showOnlyAvailable}
              onChange={(e) => setShowOnlyAvailable(e.target.checked)}
            />
            רק זמינים
          </label>
        </div>
        
        {/* Grid */}
        <div className="panel-body">
          {error && (
            <div className="error-banner">
              ❌ {error}
              <button onClick={fetchFlights}>נסה שוב</button>
            </div>
          )}
          
          <div 
            className="ag-theme-alpine" 
            style={{ height: '100%', width: '100%' }}
          >
            <AgGridReact<Flight>
              rowData={flights}
              columnDefs={columnDefs}
              defaultColDef={defaultColDef}
              onCellValueChanged={onCellValueChanged}
              rowSelection="single"
              animateRows={true}

export default function InventoryPanel({ open, onClose, onSelectFlight }: InventoryPanelProps) {
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('')
  const [flights] = useState<Flight[]>(sampleFlights)

  const filteredFlights = flights.filter(f => 
    f.destination_name.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
    f.airline.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase())
  )

  return (
    <>
      {/* Overlay */}
      <div 
        className={`panel-overlay ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}
        onClick={onClose}
      />
      
      {/* Panel */}
      <div className={`panel ${open ? 'open' : ''}`}>
        <div className="panel-header">
          <h2 className="panel-title">📦 מלאי טיסות</h2>
          <button className="panel-close" onClick={onClose}>×</button>
        </div>
        
        {/* Filters */}
        <div className="panel-filters">
          <input
            type="text"
            className="input search-input"
            placeholder="חיפוש..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        
        {/* Simple Table */}
        <div className="panel-body">
          <table className="flights-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>יעד</th>
                <th>חברה</th>
                <th>תאריך</th>
                <th>מושבים</th>
                <th>מחיר</th>
                <th></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {filteredFlights.map(flight => (
                <tr key={flight.id}>
                  <td><strong>{flight.destination_name}</strong> ({flight.destination_code})</td>
                  <td>{flight.airline}</td>
                  <td>{flight.departure_date}</td>
                  <td>{flight.available_seats}</td>
                  <td>${flight.selling_price}</td>
                  <td>
                    <button 
                      className="btn btn-primary btn-sm"
                      onClick={() => onSelectFlight(flight)}
                    >
                      + הוסף
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
          
          {filteredFlights.length === 0 && (
            <div className="empty-state">אין טיסות להצגה</div>
          )}
        </div>
        
        {/* Footer */}
        <div className="panel-footer">
          <span>{filteredFlights.length} טיסות</span>
          <div className="panel-footer-actions">
            <button className="btn btn-outline">📥 יבוא CSV</button>
            <button className="btn btn-outline">📤 ייצוא</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

    set(state => ({ flights: [...state.flights, flight] }))
    return flight
  },
  
  updateFlight: async (id, updates) => {
    const flight = await db.flights.update(id, updates)
    set(state => ({
      flights: state.flights.map(f => f.id === id ? flight : f)
    }))
    return flight
  },
  
  deleteFlight: async (id) => {
    await db.flights.delete(id)
    set(state => ({
      flights: state.flights.filter(f => f.id !== id)
    }))
  },
  
  subscribeToChanges: () => {
    const channel = subscribeToFlights((payload) => {
      const { eventType, new: newRecord, old: oldRecord } = payload
      
      set(state => {
        switch (eventType) {
          case 'INSERT':
            return { flights: [...state.flights, newRecord as Flight] }
          case 'UPDATE':
            return {
              flights: state.flights.map(f => 
                f.id === (newRecord as Flight).id ? newRecord as Flight : f
              )
            }
          case 'DELETE':
            return {
              flights: state.flights.filter(f => f.id !== (oldRecord as Flight).id)
            }
          default:
            return state
        }
      })
    })
    
    // Return unsubscribe function
    return () => {
      supabase.removeChannel(channel)
    }
  },
  new_string:   addFlight: async (flightData) => {

+import { create } from 'zustand'
+import type { Flight } from '../types/database'
+// import { db, subscribeToFlights, supabase } from '../lib/supabase'
+
+interface InventoryState {
+  flights: Flight[]
+  isLoading: boolean
+  error: string | null
+  
+  // Filters
+  searchQuery: string
+  airlineFilter: string | null
+  destinationFilter: string | null
+  dateRange: { start: string | null; end: string | null }
+  showOnlyAvailable: boolean
+  
+  // Actions
+  fetchFlights: () => Promise<void>
+  setSearchQuery: (query: string) => void
+  setAirlineFilter: (airline: string | null) => void
+  setDestinationFilter: (destination: string | null) => void
+  setDateRange: (range: { start: string | null; end: string | null }) => void
+  setShowOnlyAvailable: (show: boolean) => void
+  
+  // CRUD
+  addFlight: (flight: Omit<Flight, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'>) => Promise<Flight>
+  updateFlight: (id: string, updates: Partial<Flight>) => Promise<Flight>
+  deleteFlight: (id: string) => Promise<void>
+  
+  // Real-time
+  subscribeToChanges: () => () => void
+  
+  // Computed
+  getFilteredFlights: () => Flight[]
+  getUniqueAirlines: () => string[]
+  getUniqueDestinations: () => string[]
+}
+
+export const useInventoryStore = create<InventoryState>((set, get) => ({
+  flights: [],
+  isLoading: false,
+  error: null,
+  
+  searchQuery: '',
+  airlineFilter: null,
+  destinationFilter: null,
+  dateRange: { start: null, end: null },
+  showOnlyAvailable: true,
+  
+  fetchFlights: async () => {
+    set({ isLoading: true, error: null })
+    try {
+      // Mock data for now - will use Supabase when configured
+      set({ flights: [], isLoading: false })
+    } catch (error) {
+      set({ 
+        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to fetch flights',
+        isLoading: false 
+      })
+    }
+  },
+  
+  setSearchQuery: (query) => set({ searchQuery: query }),
+  setAirlineFilter: (airline) => set({ airlineFilter: airline }),
+  setDestinationFilter: (destination) => set({ destinationFilter: destination }),
+  setDateRange: (range) => set({ dateRange: range }),
+  setShowOnlyAvailable: (show) => set({ showOnlyAvailable: show }),
+  
+  addFlight: async (flightData) => {
+    // Mock - will use Supabase when configured
+    const flight = { ...flightData, id: Date.now().toString(), created_at: new Date().toISOString(), updated_at: new Date().toISOString() } as Flight
+    set(state => ({ flights: [...state.flights, flight] }))
+    return flight
+  },
+  
+  updateFlight: async (id, updates) => {
+    // Mock - will use Supabase when configured
+    let updatedFlight: Flight | null = null
+    set(state => ({
+      flights: state.flights.map(f => {
+        if (f.id === id) {
+          updatedFlight = { ...f, ...updates, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }
+          return updatedFlight
+        }
+        return f
+      })
+    }))
+    return updatedFlight!
+  },
+  
+  deleteFlight: async (id) => {
+    // Mock - will use Supabase when configured
+    set(state => ({
+      flights: state.flights.filter(f => f.id !== id)
+    }))
+  },
+  
+  subscribeToChanges: () => {
+    // Mock - will use Supabase real-time when configured
+    return () => {}
+  },
+  
+  getFilteredFlights: () => {
+    const { 
+      flights, 
+      searchQuery, 
+      airlineFilter, 
+      destinationFilter, 
+      dateRange,
+      showOnlyAvailable 
+    } = get()
+    
+    return flights.filter(flight => {
+      // Available seats filter
+      if (showOnlyAvailable && flight.available_seats <= 0) {
+        return false
+      }
+      
+      // Search query
+      if (searchQuery) {
+        const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase()
+        const searchFields = [
+          flight.airline,
+          flight.destination_name,
+          flight.destination_code,
+          flight.outbound_details,
+          flight.inbound_details
+        ].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase()
+        
+        if (!searchFields.includes(query)) {
+          return false
+        }
+      }
+      
+      // Airline filter
+      if (airlineFilter && flight.airline !== airlineFilter) {
+        return false
+      }
+      
+      // Destination filter
+      if (destinationFilter && flight.destination_code !== destinationFilter) {
+        return false
+      }
+      
+      // Date range filter
+      if (dateRange.start && flight.departure_date < dateRange.start) {
+        return false
+      }
+      if (dateRange.end && flight.departure_date > dateRange.end) {
+        return false
+      }
+      
+      return true
+    })
+  },
+  
+  getUniqueAirlines: () => {
+    const { flights } = get()
+    return [...new Set(flights.map(f => f.airline))].sort()
+  },
+  
+  getUniqueDestinations: () => {
+    const { flights } = get()
+    return [...new Set(flights.map(f => f.destination_code))].sort()
+  }
+}))

So the architecture should be:
- Keep the existing `index.html` (Quote Generator) unchanged
- Create a new `inventory.html` or `/inventory` page for the Inventory Manager
- Both share the same Supabase database
- Simple navigation between them (links in header)This is much simpler than what I was building. The React app I created was trying to merge everything into one SPA, which is NOT what the user wanted.

[Tool call] CreatePlan
  name: Two System Architecture
  overview: Keep the existing Quote Generator (index.html) as-is and create a separate Inventory Manager page, both sharing the same Supabase backend with simple navigation between them.
  plan: # Two Separate Systems on Same Website

## Architecture Overview

```mermaid
flowchart TB
    subgraph Website [Same Website]
        QuoteGen[index.html - Quote Generator]
        InventoryMgr[inventory.html - Inventory Manager]
    end
    
    subgraph Backend [Supabase Backend]
        DB[(PostgreSQL)]
        Auth[Authentication]
    end
    
    QuoteGen -->|Read inventory| DB
    InventoryMgr -->|CRUD inventory| DB
    QuoteGen <-->|Navigation link| InventoryMgr
```

## What Stays the Same

- [index.html](index.html) - Existing Quote Generator remains **completely unchanged**
- [view.html](view.html) - Existing public view page stays as-is
- [gmail-to-drive-script.js](gmail-to-drive-script.js) - Stays as-is

## What Gets Added

### 1. New Inventory Manager Page (`inventory.html`)

A standalone HTML page (like your existing index.html) that:
- Manages flight inventory (add, edit, delete flights)
- Uses Supabase for data storage
- Has Excel-like inline editing
- Supports CSV import from your existing sample_inventory.csv format
- Uses the same Gold/Navy theme as the Quote Generator

### 2. Navigation Between Systems

Add a simple header link in both pages:

**In index.html (Quote Generator):**
```html
<a href="inventory.html">Inventory Manager</a>
```

**In inventory.html (Inventory Manager):**
```html
<a href="index.html">Quote Generator</a>
```

### 3. Shared Supabase Backend

Both systems connect to the same Supabase project:
- Quote Generator reads from `flights` table to show available inventory
- Inventory Manager has full CRUD access to `flights` table

## File Structure After Implementation

```
lord-tickets-app/
├── index.html              # Quote Generator (UNCHANGED)
├── view.html               # Public view (UNCHANGED)
├── inventory.html          # NEW: Inventory Manager
├── gmail-to-drive-script.js
├── README.md
└── supabase/
    └── migrations/         # Database schema (already created)
```

## Inventory Manager Features

The `inventory.html` page will include:

1. **Flight Table** - Editable table showing all flights
   - Inline editing for seats, prices
   - Sort by date, airline, destination
   - Filter by airline, availability
   - Search functionality

2. **Add Flight Form** - Manual entry or AI parsing
   - Paste text to extract flight data (uses Gemini)
   - Upload image to extract (uses Gemini)
   - Manual form entry

3. **CSV Import/Export**
   - Import from your existing CSV format
   - Export updated inventory

4. **Connection to Supabase**
   - Real-time sync across users
   - Secure authentication

## Implementation Approach

Since your existing Quote Generator is vanilla HTML/JS (no React), the Inventory Manager will also be **vanilla HTML/JS** to maintain consistency and simplicity. No build step required - just open in browser.

## Migration from Python Script

The existing [flight-inventory/app.py](flight-inventory/app.py) functionality will be replicated in `inventory.html`:
- Gemini API for text/image parsing
- CSV import with same column format
- Airline normalization
- Duplicate detection

  todos: [{"id":"create-inventory-html","content":"Create inventory.html with flight management UI (table, forms, filters)"},{"id":"add-supabase-integration","content":"Add Supabase client to inventory.html for CRUD operations"},{"id":"add-gemini-parser","content":"Add Gemini API integration for text/image flight parsing"},{"id":"add-csv-import","content":"Add CSV import functionality matching existing format"},{"id":"add-navigation","content":"Add navigation links between index.html and inventory.html"},{"id":"cleanup-web-folder","content":"Remove the React web/ folder (not needed for this approach)"}]

    <script>
        // ==========================================
        // State Management
        // ==========================================
        let flights = [];
        let filteredFlights = [];
        let sortColumn = 'departure_date';
        let sortDirection = 'asc';
        let editingFlightId = null;
        let csvData = null;
        let uploadedImage = null;
        
        // Airline aliases for normalization
        const AIRLINE_ALIASES = {
            'wizz': 'Wizz Air',
            'wizz air': 'Wizz Air',
            'wizzair': 'Wizz Air',
            'w6': 'Wizz Air',
            'ryanair': 'Ryanair',
            'ryan air': 'Ryanair',
            'fr': 'Ryanair',
            'easyjet': 'easyJet',
            'easy jet': 'easyJet',
            'u2': 'easyJet',
            'elal': 'El Al',
            'el al': 'El Al',
            'ly': 'El Al',
        };
        
        // ==========================================
        // Initialization
        // ==========================================
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            loadSettings();
            loadFlights();
        });
        
        function loadSettings() {
            const geminiKey = localStorage.getItem('geminiApiKey') || '';
            const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl') || '';
            const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey') || '';
            
            document.getElementById('geminiApiKey').value = geminiKey;
            document.getElementById('geminiApiKeySettings').value = geminiKey;
            document.getElementById('supabaseUrl').value = supabaseUrl;
            document.getElementById('supabaseKey').value = supabaseKey;
        }
        
        function saveSettings() {
            const geminiKey = document.getElementById('geminiApiKeySettings').value.trim();
            const supabaseUrl = document.getElementById('supabaseUrl').value.trim();
            const supabaseKey = document.getElementById('supabaseKey').value.trim();
            
            localStorage.setItem('geminiApiKey', geminiKey);
            localStorage.setItem('supabaseUrl', supabaseUrl);
            localStorage.setItem('supabaseKey', supabaseKey);
            
            document.getElementById('geminiApiKey').value = geminiKey;
            
            closeModal('settingsModal');
            showToast('הגדרות נשמרו בהצלחה', 'success');
            
            // Reload data with new settings
            loadFlights();
        }
        
        function toggleSettings() {
            openModal('settingsModal');
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // Data Loading (Mock for now, Supabase later)
        // ==========================================
        async function loadFlights() {
            showLoading(true);
            
            try {
                // Check if Supabase is configured
                const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl');
                const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey');
                
                if (supabaseUrl && supabaseKey) {
                    // Load from Supabase
                    const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights?select=*&order=departure_date.asc`, {
                        headers: {
                            'apikey': supabaseKey,
                            'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`
                        }
                    });
                    
                    if (response.ok) {
                        flights = await response.json();
                    } else {
                        throw new Error('Failed to load from Supabase');
                    }
                } else {
                    // Load sample data
                    flights = getSampleFlights();
                }
                
                updateFilters();
                filterFlights();
                updateStats();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error loading flights:', error);
                showToast('שגיאה בטעינת נתונים', 'error');
                flights = getSampleFlights();
                filterFlights();
                updateStats();
            } finally {
                showLoading(false);
            }
        }

        async function refreshData() {
            await loadFlights();
            showToast('נתונים רועננו', 'success');
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // Rendering
        // ==========================================
        function renderFlights() {
            const tbody = document.getElementById('flightsTableBody');
            
            if (filteredFlights.length === 0) {
                tbody.innerHTML = `
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="9">
                            <div class="empty-state">
                                <span class="material-icons">flight_takeoff</span>
                                <h3>אין טיסות להצגה</h3>
                                <p>הוסף טיסה חדשה או שנה את הסינון</p>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `;
                return;
            }
            
            tbody.innerHTML = filteredFlights.map(flight => `
                <tr data-id="${flight.id}">
                    <td>${formatDateRange(flight.departure_date, flight.return_date)}</td>
                    <td>
                        <strong>${flight.destination_name}</strong>
                        <br><small style="color: var(--gray-dark);">${flight.destination_code}</small>
                    </td>
                    <td><span class="airline-badge ${getAirlineClass(flight.airline)}">${flight.airline}</span></td>
                    <td style="font-size: 0.85rem;">${flight.outbound_details || '-'}</td>
                    <td style="font-size: 0.85rem;">${flight.inbound_details || '-'}</td>
                    <td>
                        <span class="seats-badge ${getSeatsClass(flight.available_seats)}" 
                              contenteditable="true" 
                              onblur="updateSeats('${flight.id}', this.textContent)"
                              onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();this.blur();}"
                        >${flight.available_seats}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="cost-cell">
                        <span contenteditable="true" 
                              class="editable"
                              onblur="updateCost('${flight.id}', this.textContent)"
                              onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();this.blur();}"
                        >${flight.cost_price ? '$' + flight.cost_price : '-'}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td class="price-cell">
                        <span contenteditable="true"
                              class="editable" 
                              onblur="updatePrice('${flight.id}', this.textContent)"
                              onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter'){event.preventDefault();this.blur();}"
                        >$${flight.selling_price}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        <div class="actions-cell">
                            <button class="action-btn edit" onclick="editFlight('${flight.id}')" title="ערוך">
                                <span class="material-icons">edit</span>
                            </button>
                            <button class="action-btn delete" onclick="deleteFlight('${flight.id}')" title="מחק">
                                <span class="material-icons">delete</span>
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </td>
                </tr>
            `).join('');
            
            document.getElementById('showingCount').textContent = filteredFlights.length;
            document.getElementById('totalCount').textContent = flights.length;
        }
        
        function formatDateRange(start, end) {
            if (!start) return '-';
            const startDate = new Date(start);
            const startStr = `${startDate.getDate().toString().padStart(2,'0')}/${(startDate.getMonth()+1).toString().padStart(2,'0')}`;
            
            if (!end) return startStr;
            
            const endDate = new Date(end);
            const endStr = `${endDate.getDate().toString().padStart(2,'0')}/${(endDate.getMonth()+1).toString().padStart(2,'0')}`;
            
            return `${startStr} - ${endStr}`;
        }
        
        function getAirlineClass(airline) {
            const lower = airline.toLowerCase();
            if (lower.includes('wizz')) return 'airline-wizz';
            if (lower.includes('ryanair')) return 'airline-ryanair';
            if (lower.includes('easyjet')) return 'airline-easyjet';
            if (lower.includes('el al')) return 'airline-elal';
            return 'airline-other';
        }
        
        function getSeatsClass(seats) {
            if (seats === 0) return 'seats-none';
            if (seats <= 2) return 'seats-low';
            if (seats <= 5) return 'seats-medium';
            return 'seats-high';
        }
        
        function updateStats() {
            document.getElementById('totalFlights').textContent = flights.length;
            document.getElementById('totalSeats').textContent = flights.reduce((sum, f) => sum + (f.available_seats || 0), 0);
            document.getElementById('totalAirlines').textContent = [...new Set(flights.map(f => f.airline))].length;
        }
        
        function updateFilters() {
            const airlines = [...new Set(flights.map(f => f.airline))].sort();
            const airlineSelect = document.getElementById('airlineFilter');
            airlineSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">כל החברות</option>' + 
                airlines.map(a => `<option value="${a}">${a}</option>`).join('');
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // Filtering & Sorting
        // ==========================================
        function filterFlights() {
            const search = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.toLowerCase();
            const airline = document.getElementById('airlineFilter').value;
            const availability = document.getElementById('availabilityFilter').value;
            
            filteredFlights = flights.filter(f => {
                // Search filter
                if (search) {
                    const searchStr = `${f.destination_name} ${f.destination_code} ${f.airline} ${f.outbound_details || ''} ${f.inbound_details || ''}`.toLowerCase();
                    if (!searchStr.includes(search)) return false;
                }
                
                // Airline filter
                if (airline && f.airline !== airline) return false;
                
                // Availability filter
                if (availability === 'available' && f.available_seats <= 0) return false;
                if (availability === 'low' && (f.available_seats < 1 || f.available_seats > 2)) return false;
                if (availability === 'none' && f.available_seats > 0) return false;
                
                return true;
            });
            
            // Sort
            filteredFlights.sort((a, b) => {
                let valA = a[sortColumn];
                let valB = b[sortColumn];
                
                if (typeof valA === 'string') valA = valA.toLowerCase();
                if (typeof valB === 'string') valB = valB.toLowerCase();
                
                if (valA < valB) return sortDirection === 'asc' ? -1 : 1;
                if (valA > valB) return sortDirection === 'asc' ? 1 : -1;
                return 0;
            });
            
            renderFlights();
        }
        
        function sortBy(column) {
            if (sortColumn === column) {
                sortDirection = sortDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
            } else {
                sortColumn = column;
                sortDirection = 'asc';
            }
            filterFlights();
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // CRUD Operations
        // ==========================================
        function openAddModal() {
            editingFlightId = null;
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'הוסף טיסה חדשה';
            document.getElementById('flightForm').reset();
            switchTab('manual');
            openModal('flightModal');
        }
        
        function editFlight(id) {
            const flight = flights.find(f => f.id === id);
            if (!flight) return;
            
            editingFlightId = id;
            document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = 'ערוך טיסה';
            
            document.getElementById('flightId').value = id;
            document.getElementById('departureDate').value = flight.departure_date || '';
            document.getElementById('returnDate').value = flight.return_date || '';
            document.getElementById('airline').value = flight.airline || '';
            document.getElementById('destinationName').value = flight.destination_name || '';
            document.getElementById('destinationCode').value = flight.destination_code || '';
            document.getElementById('availableSeats').value = flight.available_seats || 0;
            document.getElementById('outboundDetails').value = flight.outbound_details || '';
            document.getElementById('inboundDetails').value = flight.inbound_details || '';
            document.getElementById('costPrice').value = flight.cost_price || '';
            document.getElementById('sellingPrice').value = flight.selling_price || '';
            document.getElementById('supplierInfo').value = flight.supplier_info || '';
            
            switchTab('manual');
            openModal('flightModal');
        }
        
        async function saveFlight() {
            const form = document.getElementById('flightForm');
            if (!form.checkValidity()) {
                form.reportValidity();
                return;
            }
            
            const flightData = {
                departure_date: document.getElementById('departureDate').value,
                return_date: document.getElementById('returnDate').value || null,
                airline: normalizeAirline(document.getElementById('airline').value),
                destination_name: document.getElementById('destinationName').value,
                destination_code: document.getElementById('destinationCode').value.toUpperCase(),
                available_seats: parseInt(document.getElementById('availableSeats').value) || 0,
                outbound_details: document.getElementById('outboundDetails').value,
                inbound_details: document.getElementById('inboundDetails').value,
                cost_price: parseFloat(document.getElementById('costPrice').value) || null,
                selling_price: parseFloat(document.getElementById('sellingPrice').value),
                supplier_info: document.getElementById('supplierInfo').value
            };
            
            showLoading(true);
            
            try {
                const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl');
                const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey');
                
                if (supabaseUrl && supabaseKey) {
                    // Save to Supabase
                    let response;
                    if (editingFlightId) {
                        response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights?id=eq.${editingFlightId}`, {
                            method: 'PATCH',
                            headers: {
                                'apikey': supabaseKey,
                                'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
                                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                                'Prefer': 'return=representation'
                            },
                            body: JSON.stringify(flightData)
                        });
                    } else {
                        response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights`, {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: {
                                'apikey': supabaseKey,
                                'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
                                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                                'Prefer': 'return=representation'
                            },
                            body: JSON.stringify(flightData)
                        });
                    }
                    
                    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to save');
                    await loadFlights();
                } else {
                    // Save locally
                    if (editingFlightId) {
                        const index = flights.findIndex(f => f.id === editingFlightId);
                        if (index >= 0) {
                            flights[index] = { ...flights[index], ...flightData };
                        }
                    } else {
                        flightData.id = Date.now().toString();
                        flights.push(flightData);
                    }
                    updateFilters();
                    filterFlights();
                    updateStats();
                }
                
                closeModal('flightModal');
                showToast(editingFlightId ? 'טיסה עודכנה בהצלחה' : 'טיסה נוספה בהצלחה', 'success');
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error saving flight:', error);
                showToast('שגיאה בשמירת טיסה', 'error');
            } finally {
                showLoading(false);
            }
        }
        
        async function deleteFlight(id) {
            if (!confirm('האם למחוק את הטיסה?')) return;
            
            showLoading(true);
            
            try {
                const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl');
                const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey');
                
                if (supabaseUrl && supabaseKey) {
                    const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights?id=eq.${id}`, {
                        method: 'DELETE',
                        headers: {
                            'apikey': supabaseKey,
                            'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`
                        }
                    });
                    
                    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to delete');
                    await loadFlights();
                } else {
                    flights = flights.filter(f => f.id !== id);
                    updateFilters();
                    filterFlights();
                    updateStats();
                }
                
                showToast('טיסה נמחקה', 'success');
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error deleting flight:', error);
                showToast('שגיאה במחיקת טיסה', 'error');
            } finally {
                showLoading(false);
            }
        }
        
        // Inline editing
        async function updateSeats(id, value) {
            const seats = parseInt(value) || 0;
            await updateFlightField(id, 'available_seats', seats);
        }
        
        async function updateCost(id, value) {
            const cost = parseFloat(value.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || null;
            await updateFlightField(id, 'cost_price', cost);
        }
        
        async function updatePrice(id, value) {
            const price = parseFloat(value.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0;
            await updateFlightField(id, 'selling_price', price);
        }
        
        async function updateFlightField(id, field, value) {
            const flight = flights.find(f => f.id === id);
            if (!flight) return;
            
            flight[field] = value;
            
            try {
                const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl');
                const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey');
                
                if (supabaseUrl && supabaseKey) {
                    await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights?id=eq.${id}`, {
                        method: 'PATCH',
                        headers: {
                            'apikey': supabaseKey,
                            'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
                            'Content-Type': 'application/json'
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify({ [field]: value })
                    });
                }
                
                updateStats();
                renderFlights();
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error updating field:', error);
            }
        }
        
        function normalizeAirline(name) {
            if (!name) return name;
            const lower = name.toLowerCase().trim();
            return AIRLINE_ALIASES[lower] || name.trim();
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // CSV Import/Export
        // ==========================================
        function openImportModal() {
            csvData = null;
            document.getElementById('csvUpload').value = '';
            document.getElementById('csvPreview').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('importBtn').disabled = true;
            openModal('importModal');
        }
        
        function handleCSVUpload(event) {
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (e) => {
                const text = e.target.result;
                parseCSV(text);
            };
            reader.readAsText(file, 'UTF-8');
        }
        
        function parseCSV(text) {
            const lines = text.trim().split('\n');
            if (lines.length < 2) {
                showToast('קובץ CSV ריק או לא תקין', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            // Parse header
            const header = lines[0].split(',').map(h => h.trim().replace(/"/g, ''));
            
            // Map columns
            const data = [];
            for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
                const line = lines[i].trim();
                if (!line) continue;
                
                // Skip airline header rows (only first column has value)
                const values = line.split(',').map(v => v.trim().replace(/"/g, ''));
                if (values.slice(1).every(v => !v)) continue;
                
                const row = {};
                header.forEach((h, idx) => {
                    row[h] = values[idx] || '';
                });
                
                // Convert to our format
                const flight = {
                    departure_date: parseDateFromCSV(row['תאריכים'] || row['dates'] || ''),
                    return_date: parseReturnDateFromCSV(row['תאריכים'] || row['dates'] || ''),
                    outbound_details: row['הלוך (פירוט מלא)'] || row['outbound'] || '',
                    inbound_details: row['חזור (פירוט מלא)'] || row['inbound'] || '',
                    airline: normalizeAirline(row['חברה'] || row['airline'] || ''),
                    destination_name: (row['יעד'] || row['destination'] || '').replace(/\s*\([^)]+\)/, ''),
                    destination_code: extractCode(row['יעד'] || row['destination'] || ''),
                    available_seats: parseInt(row['מושבים'] || row['seats'] || '0') || 0,
                    selling_price: parsePrice(row['מחיר'] || row['price'] || '0'),
                    cost_price: null,
                    supplier_info: ''
                };
                
                if (flight.airline && flight.destination_name) {
                    data.push(flight);
                }
            }
            
            csvData = data;
            
            // Show preview
            const preview = document.getElementById('csvPreview');
            preview.style.display = 'block';
            preview.innerHTML = `
                <table>
                    <thead>
                        <tr>
                            <th>יעד</th>
                            <th>חברה</th>
                            <th>תאריך</th>
                            <th>מושבים</th>
                            <th>מחיר</th>
                        </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                        ${data.slice(0, 10).map(f => `
                            <tr>
                                <td>${f.destination_name}</td>
                                <td>${f.airline}</td>
                                <td>${f.departure_date || '-'}</td>
                                <td>${f.available_seats}</td>
                                <td>$${f.selling_price}</td>
                            </tr>
                        `).join('')}
                        ${data.length > 10 ? `<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;">... ועוד ${data.length - 10} שורות</td></tr>` : ''}
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            `;
            
            document.getElementById('importBtn').disabled = false;
            showToast(`נמצאו ${data.length} טיסות`, 'info');
        }
        
        function parseDateFromCSV(dateStr) {
            if (!dateStr) return null;
            // Format: DD/MM - DD/MM or DD/MM/YY - DD/MM/YY
            const match = dateStr.match(/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})(?:\/(\d{2,4}))?/);
            if (!match) return null;
            
            const day = match[1].padStart(2, '0');
            const month = match[2].padStart(2, '0');
            let year = match[3] || new Date().getFullYear().toString();
            if (year.length === 2) year = '20' + year;
            
            return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
        }
        
        function parseReturnDateFromCSV(dateStr) {
            if (!dateStr) return null;
            // Format: DD/MM - DD/MM
            const parts = dateStr.split('-');
            if (parts.length < 2) return null;
            
            const match = parts[1].trim().match(/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})(?:\/(\d{2,4}))?/);
            if (!match) return null;
            
            const day = match[1].padStart(2, '0');
            const month = match[2].padStart(2, '0');
            let year = match[3] || new Date().getFullYear().toString();
            if (year.length === 2) year = '20' + year;
            
            return `${year}-${month}-${day}`;
        }
        
        function extractCode(str) {
            const match = str.match(/\(([A-Z]{3})\)/);
            return match ? match[1] : '';
        }
        
        function parsePrice(str) {
            return parseFloat(str.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) || 0;
        }
        
        async function importCSV() {
            if (!csvData || csvData.length === 0) {
                showToast('אין נתונים לייבוא', 'error');
                return;
            }
            
            showLoading(true);
            
            try {
                const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl');
                const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey');
                
                if (supabaseUrl && supabaseKey) {
                    // Insert to Supabase
                    const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights`, {
                        method: 'POST',
                        headers: {
                            'apikey': supabaseKey,
                            'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
                            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                            'Prefer': 'return=representation'
                        },
                        body: JSON.stringify(csvData)
                    });
                    
                    if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to import');
                    await loadFlights();
                } else {
                    // Add locally
                    csvData.forEach(f => {
                        f.id = Date.now().toString() + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);
                        flights.push(f);
                    });
                    updateFilters();
                    filterFlights();
                    updateStats();
                }
                
                closeModal('importModal');
                showToast(`יובאו ${csvData.length} טיסות בהצלחה`, 'success');
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error importing CSV:', error);
                showToast('שגיאה בייבוא נתונים', 'error');
            } finally {
                showLoading(false);
            }
        }
        
        function exportCSV() {
            const headers = ['תאריכים', 'הלוך (פירוט מלא)', 'חזור (פירוט מלא)', 'חברה', 'יעד', 'מושבים', 'מחיר'];
            
            const rows = flights.map(f => [
                formatDateRange(f.departure_date, f.return_date),
                f.outbound_details || '',
                f.inbound_details || '',
                f.airline,
                `${f.destination_name} (${f.destination_code})`,
                f.available_seats,
                f.selling_price + '$'
            ]);
            
            const csv = [headers.join(','), ...rows.map(r => r.map(v => `"${v}"`).join(','))].join('\n');
            
            const blob = new Blob(['\ufeff' + csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
            const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            const a = document.createElement('a');
            a.href = url;
            a.download = `flights_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)}.csv`;
            a.click();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
            
            showToast('קובץ CSV יוצא בהצלחה', 'success');
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // AI Parser (Placeholder - will be implemented)
        // ==========================================
        async function parseWithAI() {
            const text = document.getElementById('aiParserText').value.trim();
            if (!text) {
                showToast('הזן טקסט לפענוח', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            
            const apiKey = document.getElementById('geminiApiKey').value.trim() || localStorage.getItem('geminiApiKey');
            if (!apiKey) {
                showToast('הזן Gemini API Key בהגדרות', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('parseTextBtn').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('parseTextBtn').innerHTML = '<span class="material-icons">hourglass_empty</span> מפענח...';
            
            try {
                const result = await callGeminiAPI(apiKey, text, false);
                displayParserResult(result);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('AI parsing error:', error);
                showToast('שגיאה בפענוח AI', 'error');
            } finally {
                document.getElementById('parseTextBtn').disabled = false;
                document.getElementById('parseTextBtn').innerHTML = '<span class="material-icons">auto_awesome</span> פענח עם AI';
            }
        }
        
        function handleImageUpload(event) {
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (e) => {
                uploadedImage = e.target.result;
                document.getElementById('previewImg').src = uploadedImage;
                document.getElementById('imagePreview').style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('parseImageBtn').style.display = 'inline-flex';
            };
            reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        }
        
        async function parseImageWithAI() {
            if (!uploadedImage) {
                showToast('העלה תמונה קודם', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            
            const apiKey = document.getElementById('geminiApiKey').value.trim() || localStorage.getItem('geminiApiKey');
            if (!apiKey) {
                showToast('הזן Gemini API Key בהגדרות', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            
            document.getElementById('parseImageBtn').disabled = true;
            document.getElementById('parseImageBtn').innerHTML = '<span class="material-icons">hourglass_empty</span> מפענח...';
            
            try {
                const result = await callGeminiAPI(apiKey, uploadedImage, true);
                displayParserResult(result);
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('AI parsing error:', error);
                showToast('שגיאה בפענוח AI', 'error');
            } finally {
                document.getElementById('parseImageBtn').disabled = false;
                document.getElementById('parseImageBtn').innerHTML = '<span class="material-icons">auto_awesome</span> פענח תמונה עם AI';
            }
        }
        
        async function callGeminiAPI(apiKey, content, isImage) {
            const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/gemini-1.5-flash:generateContent?key=${apiKey}`;
            
            const prompt = `Extract flight information from the following input. Return ONLY a valid JSON object with these exact fields:
{
    "departure_date": "YYYY-MM-DD format",
    "return_date": "YYYY-MM-DD format or null",
    "outbound_details": "HH:MM CITY (CODE) -> HH:MM CITY (CODE) FLIGHT#",
    "inbound_details": "same format or empty",
    "airline": "Airline Name",
    "destination_name": "City name",
    "destination_code": "3-letter airport code",
    "available_seats": number,
    "selling_price": number (just the number, no currency symbol)
}

If any field is not found, use null for optional fields or reasonable defaults.
Return ONLY the JSON object, no other text.`;

            let requestBody;
            if (isImage) {
                const base64Data = content.split(',')[1];
                const mimeType = content.split(';')[0].split(':')[1];
                requestBody = {
                    contents: [{
                        parts: [
                            { text: prompt },
                            { inline_data: { mime_type: mimeType, data: base64Data } }
                        ]
                    }]
                };
            } else {
                requestBody = {
                    contents: [{
                        parts: [{ text: `${prompt}\n\nInput:\n${content}` }]
                    }]
                };
            }
            
            const response = await fetch(url, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify(requestBody)
            });
            
            if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error(`API error: ${response.status}`);
            }
            
            const data = await response.json();
            const text = data.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text;
            
            if (!text) throw new Error('No response from AI');
            
            // Extract JSON from response
            const jsonMatch = text.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/);
            if (!jsonMatch) throw new Error('No JSON in response');
            
            return JSON.parse(jsonMatch[0]);
        }
        
        function displayParserResult(result) {
            const resultDiv = document.getElementById('parserResult');
            resultDiv.style.display = 'block';
            resultDiv.className = 'parser-result success';
            
            resultDiv.innerHTML = `
                <h4 style="margin-bottom: 10px; color: var(--success);">✓ פענוח הצליח!</h4>
                <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 10px; font-size: 0.9rem;">
                    <div><strong>יעד:</strong> ${result.destination_name} (${result.destination_code})</div>
                    <div><strong>חברה:</strong> ${result.airline}</div>
                    <div><strong>תאריך:</strong> ${result.departure_date || '-'}</div>
                    <div><strong>מושבים:</strong> ${result.available_seats || '-'}</div>
                    <div><strong>מחיר:</strong> $${result.selling_price || '-'}</div>
                </div>
                <button class="btn btn-primary" style="margin-top: 15px;" onclick="applyParsedData(${JSON.stringify(result).replace(/"/g, '&quot;')})">
                    <span class="material-icons">check</span>
                    השתמש בנתונים
                </button>
            `;
        }
        
        function applyParsedData(data) {
            // Switch to manual tab and fill form
            switchTab('manual');
            
            document.getElementById('departureDate').value = data.departure_date || '';
            document.getElementById('returnDate').value = data.return_date || '';
            document.getElementById('airline').value = normalizeAirline(data.airline || '');
            document.getElementById('destinationName').value = data.destination_name || '';
            document.getElementById('destinationCode').value = data.destination_code || '';
            document.getElementById('availableSeats').value = data.available_seats || 1;
            document.getElementById('outboundDetails').value = data.outbound_details || '';
            document.getElementById('inboundDetails').value = data.inbound_details || '';
            document.getElementById('sellingPrice').value = data.selling_price || '';
            
            showToast('נתונים הועתקו לטופס', 'success');
        }
        
        // ==========================================
        // UI Helpers
        // ==========================================
        function openModal(id) {
            document.getElementById(id).classList.add('visible');
        }
        
        function closeModal(id) {
            document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('visible');
        }
        
        function switchTab(tabName) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-content').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            
            document.querySelector(`.tab[onclick="switchTab('${tabName}')"]`).classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById(tabName + 'Tab').classList.add('active');
        }
        
        function showToast(message, type = 'info') {
            const container = document.getElementById('toastContainer');
            const toast = document.createElement('div');
            toast.className = `toast ${type}`;
            toast.textContent = message;
            container.appendChild(toast);
            
            setTimeout(() => {
                toast.remove();
            }, 3000);
        }
        
        function showLoading(show) {
            document.getElementById('loadingOverlay').classList.toggle('visible', show);
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

Here's the SQL to run in Supabase. Go to **SQL Editor** → **New query** and paste this:

```sql
-- Lord Tickets Database Setup
-- Run this in Supabase SQL Editor

-- Enable UUID extension
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- FLIGHTS TABLE
CREATE TABLE flights (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    departure_date DATE NOT NULL,
    return_date DATE,
    outbound_details TEXT,
    inbound_details TEXT,
    airline TEXT NOT NULL,
    destination_code TEXT NOT NULL,
    destination_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    total_seats INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    available_seats INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    cost_price DECIMAL(10, 2),
    selling_price DECIMAL(10, 2) NOT NULL,
    supplier_info TEXT,
    notes TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(),
    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW()
);

-- Indexes for performance
CREATE INDEX idx_flights_departure ON flights(departure_date);
CREATE INDEX idx_flights_airline ON flights(airline);

-- Auto-update timestamp
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN
    NEW.updated_at = NOW();
    RETURN NEW;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

CREATE TRIGGER flights_updated_at
    BEFORE UPDATE ON flights
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION update_updated_at();

-- Allow public access (for your solo use - add auth later if needed)
ALTER TABLE flights ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY "Allow all access to flights"
ON flights FOR ALL
USING (true)
WITH CHECK (true);
```

Click **Run** to execute it.

Once done, paste your **anon key** here (the `eyJ...` string from the API settings page) and I'll configure the app!

SUMMRISE FOR ME WHAT THE GOAL OF THE PROGRAM AND WHAT IT DO
