user:
<user_query>
I am refactoring "Lord Tickets" – a travel agency system currently split into three disconnected parts: a React/HTML Quote Generator, a Python/Streamlit Inventory Manager, and a Google Apps Script for image automation.

I want to consolidate this into a single, unified Web Application backed by Supabase.

Please review my current challenges and the proposed plan by answering the following Critical Architecture Questions:

1. The "Single Source of Truth" Challenge: Currently, my inventory lives in messy files and Python scripts, while quotes are generated separately.

Question: How should we structure the Supabase Schema (relationships between Inventory, Quotes, and Bookings) so that when a quote is converted to a sale, the inventory is atomically decremented, preventing "double booking" race conditions?

2. The "Public Feed" vs. "Business Secrets" Challenge: I need to expose my inventory to external partners (via API or CSV export), showing only Product Name, Availability, and Selling Price.

Question: What is the most secure architectural pattern (e.g., Supabase Views, Edge Functions, or specific RLS policies) to ensure that my Cost_Price and Supplier_Info columns are physically impossible for an external user to query or scrape?

3. The "Excel Replacement" UI Challenge: I need a "Master Inventory" screen that feels as fast and editable as Excel (inline editing of prices/stock) but saves directly to the DB.

Question: Which React Data Grid library (TanStack Table, AG Grid, etc.) combined with Supabase subscriptions offers the best balance of performance and "live" collaboration without complex overhead?

4. The "Unstructured to Structured" Migration Challenge: Currently, I use a local Python script with Gemini to parse flight text into data.

Question: In the new web architecture, where should this heavy AI parsing logic live? Should I keep a Python microservice, or can we migrate this logic to a Supabase Edge Function (TypeScript) to keep the stack unified?

5. The Workflow Unification Challenge: I want to stop context-switching.

Question: How can we design the UI flow so that an agent can seamlessly toggle between "Building a Quote" and "Checking/Fixing Inventory" without losing unsaved data in the quote form?

ASK ME QUESTIONS UNTILL WE DEFINE NEW SOULOTIONS
</user_query>

        // ==========================================
        async function openInventoryModal() {
            const modal = document.getElementById('inventoryModal');
            const loading = document.getElementById('inventoryLoading');
            const table = document.getElementById('inventoryTable');
            const empty = document.getElementById('inventoryEmpty');
            const tbody = document.getElementById('inventoryTableBody');
            
            modal.style.display = 'flex';
            loading.style.display = 'block';
            table.style.display = 'none';
            empty.style.display = 'none';
            
            try {
                const response = await fetch(`${state.supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights?available_seats=gt.0&order=departure_date.asc`, {
                    headers: {
                        'apikey': state.supabaseKey,
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${state.supabaseKey}`
                    }
                });
                
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to fetch');
                
                const flights = await response.json();
                loading.style.display = 'none';
                
                if (flights.length === 0) {
                    empty.style.display = 'block';
                    return;
                }
                
                tbody.innerHTML = flights.map(f => `
                    <tr style="border-bottom: 1px solid #eee;">
                        <td style="padding: 12px;">${formatDateRange(f.departure_date, f.return_date)}</td>
                        <td style="padding: 12px;"><strong>${f.destination_name}</strong> (${f.destination_code})</td>
                        <td style="padding: 12px;">${f.airline}</td>
                        <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">
                            <span style="background: ${f.available_seats <= 2 ? '#DC354520' : '#28A74520'}; color: ${f.available_seats <= 2 ? '#DC3545' : '#28A745'}; padding: 4px 10px; border-radius: 15px; font-weight: 600;">${f.available_seats}</span>
                        </td>
                        <td style="padding: 12px; font-weight: 600;">$${f.selling_price}</td>
                        <td style="padding: 12px; text-align: center;">
                            <button onclick="selectInventoryFlight('${encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(f))}')" style="background: #D4AF37; color: #1B365D; border: none; padding: 8px 16px; border-radius: 6px; cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600;">+ הוסף</button>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                `).join('');
                
                table.style.display = 'table';
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Error loading inventory:', error);
                loading.style.display = 'none';
                empty.style.display = 'block';
                empty.innerHTML = `<div style="font-size: 2rem;">⚠️</div><p>שגיאה בטעינת מלאי</p><a href="inventory.html" style="color: #D4AF37;">עבור לניהול מלאי</a>`;
            }
        }
        
        function closeInventoryModal() {
            document.getElementById('inventoryModal').style.display = 'none';
        }
        
        function formatDateRange(start, end) {
            if (!start) return '-';
            const startDate = new Date(start);
            const startStr = `${startDate.getDate().toString().padStart(2,'0')}/${(startDate.getMonth()+1).toString().padStart(2,'0')}`;
            if (!end) return startStr;
            const endDate = new Date(end);
            const endStr = `${endDate.getDate().toString().padStart(2,'0')}/${(endDate.getMonth()+1).toString().padStart(2,'0')}`;
            return `${startStr} - ${endStr}`;
        }
        
        function selectInventoryFlight(encodedFlight) {
            const f = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(encodedFlight));
            
            const id = 'flight_' + Date.now();
            const flight = {
                id: id,
                status: 'ready',
                file: null,
                extractedData: {
                    flights: [{
                        from: 'TLV',
                        to: f.destination_code,
                        fromCity: 'Tel Aviv',
                        toCity: f.destination_name,
                        date: f.departure_date,
                        returnDate: f.return_date,
                        departureTime: f.outbound_details ? f.outbound_details.split(' ')[0] : '',
                        arrivalTime: '',
                        airline: f.airline,
                        flightNumber: f.outbound_details ? f.outbound_details.split(' ').pop() : '',
                        duration: '',
                        outboundDetails: f.outbound_details,
                        inboundDetails: f.inbound_details
                    }]
                },
                basePrice: f.selling_price,
                markup: 0,
                price: f.selling_price,
                currency: 'USD',
                customExchangeRate: null,
                inventoryId: f.id,
                inventorySeats: f.available_seats
            };
            
            state.flights.push(flight);
            renderFlightCards();
            closeInventoryModal();
            updatePriceSummary();
            showToast(`✅ טיסה ל${f.destination_name} נוספה להצעה`);
        }

  new_string:         let supabaseClient = null;
        let realtimeChannel = null;
        
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            loadSettings();
            loadFlights();
            setupRealtime();
        });
        
        function setupRealtime() {
            const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl');
            const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey');
            
            if (!supabaseUrl || !supabaseKey || typeof supabase === 'undefined') return;
            
            try {
                supabaseClient = supabase.createClient(supabaseUrl, supabaseKey);
                
                realtimeChannel = supabaseClient
                    .channel('flights-changes')
                    .on('postgres_changes', 
                        { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'flights' },
                        (payload) => {
                            console.log('Realtime update:', payload);
                            handleRealtimeUpdate(payload);
                        }
                    )
                    .subscribe((status) => {
                        if (status === 'SUBSCRIBED') {
                            console.log('Realtime connected!');
                            showToast('🔄 סנכרון בזמן אמת פעיל', 'info');
                        }
                    });
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Realtime setup error:', error);
            }
        }
        
        function handleRealtimeUpdate(payload) {
            const { eventType, new: newRecord, old: oldRecord } = payload;
            
            if (eventType === 'INSERT') {
                // Add new flight
                flights.push(newRecord);
                showToast(`✨ טיסה חדשה נוספה: ${newRecord.destination_name}`, 'success');
            } else if (eventType === 'UPDATE') {
                // Update existing flight
                const index = flights.findIndex(f => f.id === newRecord.id);
                if (index >= 0) {
                    flights[index] = newRecord;
                }
                showToast(`📝 טיסה עודכנה: ${newRecord.destination_name}`, 'info');
            } else if (eventType === 'DELETE') {
                // Remove flight
                flights = flights.filter(f => f.id !== oldRecord.id);
                showToast(`🗑️ טיסה נמחקה`, 'warning');
            }
            
            updateFilters();
            filterFlights();
            updateStats();
        }

<user_query>
I am refactoring "Lord Tickets" – a travel agency system currently split into three disconnected parts: a React/HTML Quote Generator, a Python/Streamlit Inventory Manager, and a Google Apps Script for image automation.

I want to consolidate this into a single, unified Web Application backed by Supabase.

Please review my current challenges and the proposed plan by answering the following Critical Architecture Questions:

1. The "Single Source of Truth" Challenge: Currently, my inventory lives in messy files and Python scripts, while quotes are generated separately.

Question: How should we structure the Supabase Schema (relationships between Inventory, Quotes, and Bookings) so that when a quote is converted to a sale, the inventory is atomically decremented, preventing "double booking" race conditions?

2. The "Public Feed" vs. "Business Secrets" Challenge: I need to expose my inventory to external partners (via API or CSV export), showing only Product Name, Availability, and Selling Price.

Question: What is the most secure architectural pattern (e.g., Supabase Views, Edge Functions, or specific RLS policies) to ensure that my Cost_Price and Supplier_Info columns are physically impossible for an external user to query or scrape?

3. The "Excel Replacement" UI Challenge: I need a "Master Inventory" screen that feels as fast and editable as Excel (inline editing of prices/stock) but saves directly to the DB.

Question: Which React Data Grid library (TanStack Table, AG Grid, etc.) combined with Supabase subscriptions offers the best balance of performance and "live" collaboration without complex overhead?

4. The "Unstructured to Structured" Migration Challenge: Currently, I use a local Python script with Gemini to parse flight text into data.

Question: In the new web architecture, where should this heavy AI parsing logic live? Should I keep a Python microservice, or can we migrate this logic to a Supabase Edge Function (TypeScript) to keep the stack unified?

5. The Workflow Unification Challenge: I want to stop context-switching.

Question: How can we design the UI flow so that an agent can seamlessly toggle between "Building a Quote" and "Checking/Fixing Inventory" without losing unsaved data in the quote form?

ASK ME QUESTIONS UNTILL WE DEFINE NEW SOULOTIONS
</user_query>

user:
<user_query>
I am refactoring "Lord Tickets" – a travel agency system currently split into three disconnected parts: a React/HTML Quote Generator, a Python/Streamlit Inventory Manager, and a Google Apps Script for image automation.

I want to consolidate this into a single, unified Web Application backed by Supabase.

Please review my current challenges and the proposed plan by answering the following Critical Architecture Questions:

1. The "Single Source of Truth" Challenge: Currently, my inventory lives in messy files and Python scripts, while quotes are generated separately.

Question: How should we structure the Supabase Schema (relationships between Inventory, Quotes, and Bookings) so that when a quote is converted to a sale, the inventory is atomically decremented, preventing "double booking" race conditions?

2. The "Public Feed" vs. "Business Secrets" Challenge: I need to expose my inventory to external partners (via API or CSV export), showing only Product Name, Availability, and Selling Price.

Question: What is the most secure architectural pattern (e.g., Supabase Views, Edge Functions, or specific RLS policies) to ensure that my Cost_Price and Supplier_Info columns are physically impossible for an external user to query or scrape?

3. The "Excel Replacement" UI Challenge: I need a "Master Inventory" screen that feels as fast and editable as Excel (inline editing of prices/stock) but saves directly to the DB.

Question: Which React Data Grid library (TanStack Table, AG Grid, etc.) combined with Supabase subscriptions offers the best balance of performance and "live" collaboration without complex overhead?

4. The "Unstructured to Structured" Migration Challenge: Currently, I use a local Python script with Gemini to parse flight text into data.

Question: In the new web architecture, where should this heavy AI parsing logic live? Should I keep a Python microservice, or can we migrate this logic to a Supabase Edge Function (TypeScript) to keep the stack unified?

5. The Workflow Unification Challenge: I want to stop context-switching.

Question: How can we design the UI flow so that an agent can seamlessly toggle between "Building a Quote" and "Checking/Fixing Inventory" without losing unsaved data in the quote form?

ASK ME QUESTIONS UNTILL WE DEFINE NEW SOULOTIONS
</user_query>

  contents: # Lord Tickets - Travel Agency System

A unified travel agency system with two separate modules: **Quote Generator** and **Inventory Manager**, both connected to a shared Supabase database.

## Live Demo

- GitHub Pages: https://shakedlisha.github.io/lord-tickets-app/
- Custom Domain: https://www.supatours.com/lord-tickets-app/

## System Architecture

```
lord-tickets-app/
├── index.html          # Quote Generator - Create customer quotes
├── inventory.html      # Inventory Manager - Manage flight inventory
├── view.html           # Public view for shared quotes
└── supabase/
    └── migrations/     # Database schema
```

## Two Systems, One Database

### 1. Quote Generator (`index.html`)

Create professional travel quotes for customers:
- Add flights from inventory or manually
- Add hotels and services
- AI-powered image parsing (Gemini)
- Calculate prices with commission
- Generate shareable links
- Export to WhatsApp format

### 2. Inventory Manager (`inventory.html`)

Manage your flight inventory:
- Add, edit, delete flights
- Inline editing (Excel-like)
- Real-time sync across users
- CSV import/export
- AI text/image parsing (Gemini)
- Filter by airline, availability
- Search functionality

## Features

| Feature | Quote Generator | Inventory Manager |
|---------|----------------|-------------------|
| Supabase Integration | Read inventory | Full CRUD |
| AI Parsing (Gemini) | Images | Text & Images |
| Real-time Sync | - | Yes |
| CSV Import/Export | - | Yes |
| Hebrew RTL Support | Yes | Yes |
| Gold/Navy Theme | Yes | Yes |

## Quick Start

1. **Open in Browser**: Just open `index.html` or `inventory.html` directly
2. **No Build Required**: Pure HTML/CSS/JS - no npm, no bundler
3. **Navigation**: Click the header links to switch between systems

## Supabase Setup (Optional)

To enable cloud storage and sync:

1. Create a project at [supabase.com](https://supabase.com)
2. Go to SQL Editor and run the migrations in `supabase/migrations/`
3. Copy your Project URL and Anon Key
4. Enter them in the Settings (gear icon) in both pages

### Database Schema

```sql
-- Main flights table
CREATE TABLE flights (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    departure_date DATE,
    return_date DATE,
    airline TEXT,
    destination_code TEXT,
    destination_name TEXT,
    available_seats INTEGER,
    selling_price DECIMAL,
    cost_price DECIMAL,      -- Private
    supplier_info TEXT       -- Private
);
```

## Gemini AI Setup

For AI-powered text/image parsing:

1. Get an API key from [Google AI Studio](https://makersuite.google.com/app/apikey)
2. Enter the key in the Gemini API field in the header

## Tech Stack

- **Frontend**: Vanilla HTML, CSS, JavaScript
- **Database**: Supabase (PostgreSQL)
- **AI**: Google Gemini API
- **Fonts**: Heebo (Hebrew), Material Icons
- **No Build Tools**: Open directly in browser

## File Descriptions

| File | Description |
|------|-------------|
| `index.html` | Quote Generator - main app for creating customer quotes |
| `inventory.html` | Inventory Manager - manage flight stock |
| `view.html` | Public view page for shared quotes |
| `gmail-to-drive-script.js` | Google Apps Script for email automation |
| `SETUP_GMAIL_IMPORT.md` | Instructions for Gmail import setup |

## Development

No setup needed! Just edit the HTML files and refresh your browser.

```bash
# Open Quote Generator
start index.html

# Open Inventory Manager
start inventory.html
```

## License

Private project for Lord Tickets travel agency.

  old_string:         function handleCSVUpload(event) {
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            
            const reader = new FileReader();
            reader.onload = (e) => {
                const text = e.target.result;
                parseCSV(text);
            };
            reader.readAsText(file, 'UTF-8');
        }
  new_string:         function handleFileUpload(event) {
            const file = event.target.files[0];
            if (!file) return;
            
            const fileName = file.name.toLowerCase();
            
            if (fileName.endsWith('.xlsx') || fileName.endsWith('.xls')) {
                // Handle Excel file
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = (e) => {
                    try {
                        const data = new Uint8Array(e.target.result);
                        const workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: 'array' });
                        const firstSheet = workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]];
                        const csvText = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_csv(firstSheet);
                        parseCSV(csvText);
                        showToast('קובץ Excel נטען בהצלחה', 'success');
                    } catch (error) {
                        console.error('Excel parse error:', error);
                        showToast('שגיאה בקריאת קובץ Excel', 'error');
                    }
                };
                reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
            } else {
                // Handle CSV file
                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = (e) => {
                    const text = e.target.result;
                    parseCSV(text);
                };
                reader.readAsText(file, 'UTF-8');
            }
        }
        
        // Keep old function name for backwards compatibility
        function handleCSVUpload(event) {
            handleFileUpload(event);
        }

  path: c:\Users\shake\lord-tickets-app\inventory.html
  old_string:         document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {

  new_string:         document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            loadSettings();

            setupRealtime();
            setupPasteHandler();
        });
        
        // Ctrl+V Paste Screenshot Handler
        function setupPasteHandler() {
            document.addEventListener('paste', async (event) => {
                const items = event.clipboardData?.items;
                if (!items) return;
                
                for (const item of items) {
                    if (item.type.startsWith('image/')) {
                        event.preventDefault();
                        const file = item.getAsFile();
                        if (file) {
                            await handlePastedImage(file);
                        }
                        break;
                    }
                }
            });
        }
        
        async function handlePastedImage(file) {
            const apiKey = document.getElementById('geminiApiKey').value.trim() || localStorage.getItem('geminiApiKey');
            if (!apiKey) {
                showToast('הזן Gemini API Key בהגדרות כדי לפענח תמונות', 'warning');
                return;
            }
            
            showToast('📷 מפענח תמונה...', 'info');
            showLoading(true);
            
            try {
                // Convert file to base64
                const base64 = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
                    const reader = new FileReader();
                    reader.onload = () => resolve(reader.result);
                    reader.onerror = reject;
                    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
                });
                
                // Call Gemini API
                const result = await callGeminiAPI(apiKey, base64, true);
                
                if (result) {
                    // Add the parsed flight directly
                    await addFlightFromParsedData(result);
                    showToast(`✅ טיסה ל${result.destination_name || 'יעד'} נוספה בהצלחה!`, 'success');
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error('Paste image error:', error);
                showToast('שגיאה בפענוח התמונה', 'error');
            } finally {
                showLoading(false);
            }
        }
        
        async function addFlightFromParsedData(data) {
            const flightData = {
                departure_date: data.departure_date || null,
                return_date: data.return_date || null,
                airline: normalizeAirline(data.airline || ''),
                destination_name: data.destination_name || '',
                destination_code: data.destination_code || '',
                available_seats: parseInt(data.available_seats) || 1,
                total_seats: parseInt(data.available_seats) || 1,
                outbound_details: data.outbound_details || '',
                inbound_details: data.inbound_details || '',
                cost_price: null,
                selling_price: parseFloat(data.selling_price) || 0,
                supplier_info: ''
            };
            
            const supabaseUrl = localStorage.getItem('supabaseUrl');
            const supabaseKey = localStorage.getItem('supabaseKey');
            
            if (supabaseUrl && supabaseKey) {
                // Save to Supabase
                const response = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/rest/v1/flights`, {
                    method: 'POST',
                    headers: {
                        'apikey': supabaseKey,
                        'Authorization': `Bearer ${supabaseKey}`,
                        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                        'Prefer': 'return=representation'
                    },
                    body: JSON.stringify(flightData)
                });
                
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Failed to save');
                await loadFlights();
            } else {
                // Save locally
                flightData.id = Date.now().toString();
                flights.push(flightData);
                updateFilters();
                filterFlights();
                updateStats();
            }
        }
